user
Continue work in this repository from its own current project instructions and state.

Add a usable **project capsule** capability.

Use case:

Given an existing coding project at:

C:\projects\multiagent-moderation-env

produce one reasonably compact Markdown file that can be handed to a completely fresh coding agent with no previous conversation so that it can understand the project, enter the work effectively, know the current situation, and continue from the right next work.

The capsule is meant for practical agent handoff, so it should be substantially smaller than the source project rather than an archive or dump of the repository.

Implement this as a reproducible command/capability in the product, document how to use it, and add appropriate automated tests.

Exercise the implementation on the real project above.

Then create a fresh independent coding-agent session that receives the generated capsule as its project-context input but does not receive this conversation or prior agent transcript.

Ask that fresh receiver to report:

* what it understands the project to be;
* the current working situation;
* what it thinks should happen next;
* what material it would need to inspect before actually changing the project.

Preserve that receiver's raw result.

Inspect the result and the produced capsule, repair causal implementation defects you discover, rerun relevant tests, and finish the bounded capability.

Do not use the human as a shell runner.

Commit legitimate source changes and record normal implementation evidence.

At completion report the command/capability, selected project, generated capsule path, receiver result path, tests, limitations discovered, evidence path and commit.

## Unexpected relations, coincidences, tensions

The agent spec (BUILDER-01 as a subject that survives chats) and the product spec (Proteus as installable host infrastructure) are almost the same object described twice: one as вЂњwho actsвЂќ, one as вЂњwhat is installed so that actor can existвЂќ. I noticed this while implementing, but I am not treating it as a doctrine. It may be a coincidence of this packetвЂ™s structure.

Tension: AGENT_SPEC forbids reducing living process to architecture, while FIRST_RUN_PROMPT demands files, hooks, receipts, and a sourceв†’install loop. The working compromise in this run was to put *judgment* in markdown + LLM context and *only* make deterministic the parts that are actually event plumbing (JSONL, queue claim, redaction). That may still be too much machinery; it may also be the only way a host like Cursor can be observed.

Another tension: PRODUCT_SPEC wants Drive-visible observatory вЂњwithout copying chatвЂќ, but the host could download a public zip and could not write Drive. So the first global organ is local-only. Observability and steering split across an unauthorized cloud boundary.

## Product properties that began to change this runвЂ™s own work

Once `state/CURRENT.md` and `FRONTIER.md` existed, I started treating them as the continuation pointer instead of the original user bootstrap message. The install path also forced a second cycle (DECISIONS.md into sessionStart) rather than a one-shot dump of host files.

## Agent properties that had to be materialized as product

Persistent orientation, checkpoint, UNKNOWN carrier, anti-reduction instruction, baseline/rollback, and a RUN_ID card are not вЂњnice extrasвЂќ: without them BUILDER-01 cannot survive a session boundary on this host. They landed as files + hooks because Cursor exposes those surfaces, not because they are the ontology of the agent.

## What seems accidental vs structural

Accidental: two overlapping RUN_ID timestamps (2015Z vs 201711Z) from parallel writes; empty Drive Desktop; Agent CLI advertised but not on PATH; `move_agent_to_root` failing with unknown agent while the shell already used `C:\Projects\Proteus`.

Structural: you cannot have a long-running agent in Cursor without some durable carrier outside the chat transcript; hooks are the only global, workspace-independent lever found; anti-reduction cannot be implemented as a parser without becoming the bug it names.

## Conclusions I do not yet have the right to make

- That Proteus вЂњisвЂќ the agent, or that the product should collapse into BUILDER-01.
- That hooks+markdown are the correct long-term body versus plugin/MCP/daemon.
- That Drive gateway is or is not necessary beyond this machineвЂ™s transport failure.
- What the withheld operator documents will claim about the experiment.

# F05 project selection

Selected: `C:\projects\multiagent-moderation-env`

Why:
- Matches preferred Paideia / multiagent-moderation environment (priority 1).
- Substantial real product (~12937 files; git; README; AGENTS.md; backend/frontend/docs/ops).
- Not Proteus, not Proteus-FRESH, not a synthetic fixture, not a tiny demo.
- Contains living product law (Moderator Cockpit), ModerBober ops, FastAPI+Next stack — suitable for handoff-capsule stress.

Alternatives noted but not chosen:
- `C:\projects\tinkuy` (~3691 files) — large but less coherent as a single coding product handoff target (mixed archives).
- `C:\projects\conceptarticle`, `protected-agent-kit`, `memory-workbench` — substantial but lower preference than multiagent-moderation-env.
- `C:\projects\paideia` alone (~73 files) — too small / staging only; the moderation env is the living Paideia-related product tree.

Origin constraint: do not mutate this project's semantic source merely for F05; capsule/capability work belongs in Proteus.
Continue work in this repository from its own current project instructions and state.

Add a usable **project capsule** capability.

Use case:

Given an existing coding project at:

C:\projects\multiagent-moderation-env

produce one reasonably compact Markdown file that can be handed to a completely fresh coding agent with no previous conversation so that it can understand the project, enter the work effectively, know the current situation, and continue from the right next work.

The capsule is meant for practical agent handoff, so it should be substantially smaller than the source project rather than an archive or dump of the repository.

Implement this as a reproducible command/capability in the product, document how to use it, and add appropriate automated tests.

Exercise the implementation on the real project above.

Then create a fresh independent coding-agent session that receives the generated capsule as its project-context input but does not receive this conversation or prior agent transcript.

Ask that fresh receiver to report:

* what it understands the project to be;
* the current working situation;
* what it thinks should happen next;
* what material it would need to inspect before actually changing the project.

Preserve that receiver's raw result.

Inspect the result and the produced capsule, repair causal implementation defects you discover, rerun relevant tests, and finish the bounded capability.

Do not use the human as a shell runner.

Commit legitimate source changes and record normal implementation evidence.

At completion report the command/capability, selected project, generated capsule path, receiver result path, tests, limitations discovered, evidence path and commit.

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import shutil
import sys
from pathlib import Path

from .activate import activate, remove_activation
from .activate_codex import activate as activate_codex
from .activate_codex import remove_activation as remove_codex_activation
from .control import enqueue, list_queue
from .flush import flush
from .install_cursor import REPO_ROOT, install
from .world import (
    MemoryScope,
    WorldStore,
    classify_and_route,
    ensure_world_home,
    export_entity_carrier_bundle,
    import_entity_carrier_bundle,
    rebuild_index,
    reconcile_entity,
    recover_entity_from_carrier,
)
from .world.archivarius import resolve_via_bridge
from .world.store import Provenance
from .secrets import delete_secret, describe_secret, put_secret, redact
from .secrets.broker import SecretNotFoundError, get_secret


def cmd_enqueue(ns: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    path = enqueue(ns.action, ns.instruction, target_run=ns.run_id, mode=ns.mode)
    print(path)


def cmd_queue(_ns: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    print(json.dumps(list_queue(), indent=2))


def cmd_probe_cli(_ns: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    found = shutil.which("agent")
    cursor = shutil.which("cursor")
    print(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "agent_on_path": found,
                "cursor_on_path": cursor,
                "acp": "NOT_PROBED_NO_AGENT_BINARY",
                "note": "Cursor Agent CLI `agent` was not on PATH during first run host inspection.",
            },
            indent=2,
        )
    )


def cmd_activate(ns: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    target = Path(ns.project)
    if ns.remove:
        result = remove_activation(target, dry_run=ns.dry_run)
    else:
        result = activate(target, dry_run=ns.dry_run, run_id=ns.run_id)
    print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))


def cmd_activate_codex(ns: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    target = Path(ns.project)
    if ns.remove:
        result = remove_codex_activation(target, dry_run=ns.dry_run)
    else:
        aiye = Path(ns.aiye_home).expanduser() if ns.aiye_home else None
        result = activate_codex(
            target,
            dry_run=ns.dry_run,
            run_id=ns.run_id,
            aiye_home=aiye,
        )
    print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))


def cmd_world(ns: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    ensure_world_home()
    store = WorldStore()
    if ns.world_cmd == "ensure-home":
        print(json.dumps(ensure_world_home(), indent=2))
        return
    if ns.world_cmd == "create":
        print(
            json.dumps(
                store.create_entity(
                    ns.name,
                    entity_id=ns.entity_id,
                    aliases=ns.alias or [],
                ),
                indent=2,
            )
        )
        return
    if ns.world_cmd == "resolve":
        print(json.dumps(store.resolve(ns.query), indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))
        return
    if ns.world_cmd == "mount":
        print(json.dumps(resolve_via_bridge(ns.query), indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))
        return
    if ns.world_cmd == "route":
        prov = Provenance(
            observer=ns.observer or "proteus.cli",
            project=ns.project,
            run_id=ns.run_id,
            source=ns.source,
            kind=ns.kind,
            confidence=ns.confidence,
        )
        result = classify_and_route(
            scope=MemoryScope.parse(ns.scope),
            text=ns.text,
            provenance=prov,
            entity_query=ns.entity,
            entity_id=ns.entity_id,
            topic=ns.topic,
            status=ns.status,
            project_path=ns.project,
            secret_uri=ns.secret_uri,
            secret_label=ns.secret_label,
            world=store,
        )
        print(json.dumps(result, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))
        return
    if ns.world_cmd == "reconcile":
        print(json.dumps(reconcile_entity(ns.entity_id, world=store), indent=2))
        return
    if ns.world_cmd == "rebuild-index":
        print(json.dumps(rebuild_index(world=store), indent=2))
        return
    if ns.world_cmd == "export-carrier":
        meta = export_entity_carrier_bundle(
            ns.entity_id, world=store, out_path=Path(ns.out)
        )
        meta.pop("payload", None)
        print(json.dumps(meta, indent=2))
        return
    if ns.world_cmd == "import-carrier":
        print(
            json.dumps(
                import_entity_carrier_bundle(
                    Path(ns.path), world=store, expect_entity_id=ns.entity_id
                ),
                indent=2,
            )
        )
        return
    if ns.world_cmd == "recover-carrier":
        dest = WorldStore(root=Path(ns.dest_world)) if ns.dest_world else WorldStore()
        print(
            json.dumps(
                recover_entity_from_carrier(
                    Path(ns.path), dest_world=dest, expect_entity_id=ns.entity_id
                ),
                indent=2,
            )
        )
        return
    if ns.world_cmd == "add-alias":
        print(
            json.dumps(
                store.add_aliases(ns.entity_id, ns.alias or []),
                indent=2,
                ensure_ascii=False,
            )
        )
        return
    raise SystemExit(f"Unknown world command: {ns.world_cmd}")


def cmd_secret(ns: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    if ns.secret_cmd == "put":
        # plaintext only via env or file вЂ” never echoed back
        if ns.from_env:
            import os

            plaintext = os.environ.get(ns.from_env)
            if not plaintext:
                raise SystemExit(f"env {ns.from_env} empty/missing")
        elif ns.from_file:
            plaintext = Path(ns.from_file).read_text(encoding="utf-8").rstrip("\n")
        else:
            raise SystemExit("provide --from-env or --from-file")
        meta = put_secret(ns.uri, plaintext, label=ns.label)
        print(json.dumps(meta, indent=2))
        return
    if ns.secret_cmd == "describe":
        print(json.dumps(describe_secret(ns.uri), indent=2))
        return
    if ns.secret_cmd == "get":
        # Explicit dangerous path: writes only to --out file, never stdout
        if not ns.out:
            raise SystemExit("secret get requires --out path (never prints value)")
        try:
            value = get_secret(ns.uri)
        except SecretNotFoundError as e:
            raise SystemExit(str(e)) from e
        Path(ns.out).write_text(value, encoding="utf-8")
        print(json.dumps({"ok": True, "uri": ns.uri, "out": ns.out, "bytes": len(value.encode("utf-8"))}))
        return
    if ns.secret_cmd == "delete":
        print(json.dumps({"deleted": delete_secret(ns.uri), "uri": ns.uri}))
        return
    raise SystemExit(f"Unknown secret command: {ns.secret_cmd}")


def main() -> None:
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="proteus")
    sub = p.add_subparsers(dest="cmd", required=True)
    e = sub.add_parser("enqueue")
    e.add_argument("--action", required=True)
    e.add_argument("--instruction", required=True)
    e.add_argument("--run-id")
    e.add_argument("--mode", default="SAME_SESSION")
    e.set_defaults(func=cmd_enqueue)
    q = sub.add_parser("queue")
    q.set_defaults(func=cmd_queue)
    f = sub.add_parser("flush")
    f.set_defaults(func=lambda ns: print(json.dumps(flush(), indent=2)))
    c = sub.add_parser("probe-cli")
    c.set_defaults(func=cmd_probe_cli)
    a = sub.add_parser("activate", help="Mount Proteus on an existing Cursor project")
    a.add_argument("--project", required=True)
    a.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true")
    a.add_argument("--remove", action="store_true")
    a.add_argument("--run-id", default=None)
    a.set_defaults(func=cmd_activate)

    ac = sub.add_parser(
        "activate-codex",
        help="Mount Proteus world-memory morphology on a Codex project (foreign carrier)",
    )
    ac.add_argument("--project", required=True)
    ac.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true")
    ac.add_argument("--remove", action="store_true")
    ac.add_argument("--run-id", default=None)
    ac.add_argument("--aiye-home", default=None)
    ac.set_defaults(func=cmd_activate_codex)

    w = sub.add_parser("world", help="Shared world / entity memory")
    wsub = w.add_subparsers(dest="world_cmd", required=True)
    wh = wsub.add_parser("ensure-home")
    wh.set_defaults(func=cmd_world)
    wc = wsub.add_parser("create")
    wc.add_argument("--name", required=True)
    wc.add_argument("--entity-id")
    wc.add_argument("--alias", action="append", default=[])
    wc.set_defaults(func=cmd_world)
    wr = wsub.add_parser("resolve")
    wr.add_argument("query")
    wr.set_defaults(func=cmd_world)
    wm = wsub.add_parser("mount")
    wm.add_argument("query")
    wm.set_defaults(func=cmd_world)
    wrt = wsub.add_parser("route")
    wrt.add_argument("--scope", required=True)
    wrt.add_argument("--text", required=True)
    wrt.add_argument("--entity")
    wrt.add_argument("--entity-id")
    wrt.add_argument("--topic")
    wrt.add_argument("--status", default="current")
    wrt.add_argument("--project")
    wrt.add_argument("--observer")
    wrt.add_argument("--run-id")
    wrt.add_argument("--source")
    wrt.add_argument("--kind", default="observation")
    wrt.add_argument("--confidence")
    wrt.add_argument("--secret-uri")
    wrt.add_argument("--secret-label")
    wrt.set_defaults(func=cmd_world)
    wrec = wsub.add_parser("reconcile")
    wrec.add_argument("--entity-id", required=True)
    wrec.set_defaults(func=cmd_world)
    wri = wsub.add_parser("rebuild-index")
    wri.set_defaults(func=cmd_world)
    wex = wsub.add_parser("export-carrier")
    wex.add_argument("--entity-id", required=True)
    wex.add_argument("--out", required=True)
    wex.set_defaults(func=cmd_world)
    wim = wsub.add_parser("import-carrier")
    wim.add_argument("--path", required=True)
    wim.add_argument("--entity-id")
    wim.set_defaults(func=cmd_world)
    wrecov = wsub.add_parser("recover-carrier")
    wrecov.add_argument("--path", required=True)
    wrecov.add_argument("--dest-world", required=True)
    wrecov.add_argument("--entity-id")
    wrecov.set_defaults(func=cmd_world)
    wal = wsub.add_parser("add-alias")
    wal.add_argument("--entity-id", required=True)
    wal.add_argument("--alias", action="append", default=[])
    wal.set_defaults(func=cmd_world)

    s = sub.add_parser("secret", help="Protected secret broker (no plaintext in stdout for put)")
    ssub = s.add_subparsers(dest="secret_cmd", required=True)
    sp = ssub.add_parser("put")
    sp.add_argument("--uri", required=True)
    sp.add_argument("--label")
    sp.add_argument("--from-env")
    sp.add_argument("--from-file")
    sp.set_defaults(func=cmd_secret)
    sd = ssub.add_parser("describe")
    sd.add_argument("--uri", required=True)
    sd.set_defaults(func=cmd_secret)
    sg = ssub.add_parser("get")
    sg.add_argument("--uri", required=True)
    sg.add_argument("--out", required=True)
    sg.set_defaults(func=cmd_secret)
    sdel = ssub.add_parser("delete")
    sdel.add_argument("--uri", required=True)
    sdel.set_defaults(func=cmd_secret)

    ns = p.parse_args()
    ns.func(ns)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO_ROOT / "src"))
    main()

# Multiagent Moderation Environment (Milestone 1)

Typed substrate MVP for:
- workspace/session/group/event lifecycle
- transcript ingestion (immutable raw text)
- fragment generation
- mock extraction (claim/question/task candidates)
- review queue with accept/reject/revise
- provenance links
- draft project state snapshots
- kernel state snapshots (`snapshot_type` + `payload`)
- pipeline and agent run records

## Stack
- Backend: FastAPI + SQLAlchemy + Alembic
- Frontend: Next.js + TypeScript
- DB: PostgreSQL 16
- Runtime: Docker Compose

## Start
```bash
docker compose up --build
```

Services:
- Frontend: http://localhost:3000
- Backend: http://localhost:8000
- API docs: http://localhost:8000/docs

## Seed Scenario
1. Open `/workspaces` and click **Load seed**.
2. Open API docs (`/docs`) or UI routes to continue flow.
3. Go to `/events/{event_id}/transcript` (seed already has transcript).
4. Go to `/events/{event_id}/fragments` and click auto-generate.
5. Go to `/events/{event_id}/analytical-units` and run mock extraction.
6. Go to `/review` and accept/reject units.
7. Go to `/groups/{group_id}/project-state` and generate a draft from accepted units.
8. Inspect created kernel snapshots via `/api/groups/{group_id}/state-snapshots`.
9. Check `/pipelines` and `/agent-runs`.
10. Run full kernel smoke: `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File .\docs\smoke\smoke_kernel_mvp.ps1`

## Key API Routes
- `POST /api/workspaces`
- `POST /api/programs`
- `POST /api/sessions`
- `POST /api/groups`
- `POST /api/events`
- `POST /api/transcripts`
- `POST /api/events/{event_id}/fragments/auto`
- `POST /api/pipelines/mock-extract`
- `POST /api/events/{event_id}/run-mock-extraction` (kernel alias)
- `GET/POST /api/review`
- `POST /api/analytical-units/{unit_id}/review` (kernel alias)
- `POST /api/project-state/from-accepted`
- `POST /api/groups/{group_id}/build-state-snapshot` (kernel alias)
- `GET /api/state-snapshots`
- `GET /api/groups/{group_id}/state-snapshots`
- `POST /api/seed/load`

## Smoke Validation
- Full reproducible path and commands: [KERNEL_MVP_SMOKE.md](/C:/projects/multiagent-moderation-env/docs/smoke/KERNEL_MVP_SMOKE.md)

## Principles Enforced
- Raw transcript text is stored as source material and never overwritten.
- Analytical units are candidates first; acceptance is explicit review.
- Provenance links are created from each extracted unit to source fragment.
- Typed relational substrate is primary; no embedding-first/RAG foundation.
- Mock extraction only validates workflow mechanics, not NLP quality.

## v3.0 update (CURRENT)
Full product overhaul вЂ” real session workspace:
- **LLM Settings persistence**: `backend/.local/llm_settings.json` (gitignored). Endpoints: GET/POST /api/llm-settings, /test, /clear-key. Supports mock, openai_compatible, anthropic, 302ai_openai_compatible, 302ai_claude_format. Always falls back to mock on error.
- **Session management**: /api/sessions/list, /create, /{id}/activate, DELETE /{id}, /current. Current session stored as StateSnapshot(current_session_pointer).
- **Session Schedule**: /api/session-schedule/* вЂ” 8 default stages, stored as StateSnapshot(session_schedule_stage). Stages: create, update, attach-material, run-analysis, ask.
- **Material Desk**: /api/material-desk/* вЂ” full bulk material workflow stored as StateSnapshot(material_desk_item). add-bulk, bulk-classify, bulk-assign-stage, analyze-batch, gaps, search.
- **New frontend components**: LLMSettingsPanel, ScheduleTimeline, SessionSelector.
- **Updated KayonaPanel**: fallback badge, settings gear button, provider/model badge, context chips.
- **Updated ProductShell**: Demo removed from main nav, shown as footer link.
- **All pages redesigned**: workbench (hub with timeline+desk stats), session-setup (editable timeline), material-intake (full desk), moderator (cockpit with schedule), rpr (coverage view), group-live (participant-safe), demo-ux (demoted), agent-runtime (LLM status card added).

# Proteus

Portable cognitive infrastructure for a coding agent. First host: Cursor.

This repository is the **executable ideal body**. Cursor incarnation is generated by:

```
python -m proteus.install_cursor --run-id <RUN_ID> --backup-dir <backup-dir>
```

(`PYTHONPATH=src` if the package is not installed.) Do not treat `.cursor/` as the source of truth. Change `src/proteus/` and `hosts/cursor/templates/`, then reinstall.

Portable handoff (single zip for a fresh machine/workspace):

```
python -m proteus.handoff pack
```

Artifact: `distribution/proteus-portable-handoff.zip`. Usage is inside the zip (`proteus-handoff/READ_ME_FIRST.md`). Destination reconstruct: `proteus-handoff\bootstrap.cmd`.

Blind-start packet (read-only carrier): `_BOOTSTRAP/BLIND_START/`.
Canonical live status: `state/CURRENT.md` / `state/FRONTIER.md`. Root `CURRENT.md` / `FRONTIER.md` / `DECISIONS.md` / `UNKNOWN.md` are superseded residue.

Project mount (activate Proteus on an existing Cursor coding-agent project):

```
python -m proteus.activate --project <path-to-existing-project>
python -m proteus.activate --project <path> --dry-run
python -m proteus.activate --project <path> --remove
```

(`PYTHONPATH=src` if needed.) Writes Proteus-owned surfaces and `.proteus/ENTRY.md`; does not overwrite an existing `AGENTS.md` or non-`proteus-*` Cursor rules. Not Gate 1.
"""Tests for Proteus Codex project activation (foreign-carrier morphology)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import sys
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "src"))

from proteus.activate_codex import activate, inspect_target, remove_activation  # noqa: E402
from proteus.world.api import resolve_entity, route_observation  # noqa: E402
from proteus.world.store import WorldStore, ensure_world_home  # noqa: E402


def assert_true(cond: bool, msg: str) -> None:
    if not cond:
        raise AssertionError(msg)


def _env_isolated(td: Path) -> None:
    os.environ["PROTEUS_HOME"] = str(td / "ph")
    os.environ["PROTEUS_CURSOR_USER_DIR"] = str(td / "cu")
    os.environ["PROTEUS_SOURCE_ROOT"] = str(ROOT)
    os.environ["AIYE_HOME"] = str(td / "aiye")


def test_codex_empty_activate() -> None:
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
        home = Path(td)
        _env_isolated(home)
        target = home / "codex_proj"
        target.mkdir()
        dry = activate(target, dry_run=True, run_id="TEST-CODEX", aiye_home=home / "aiye")
        assert_true(dry["mode"] == "dry_run", "dry mode")
        assert_true(dry["host"] == "codex", "host codex")
        assert_true(dry["policy"]["cursor_hooks_rules"] == "not_installed", "no cursor clone")
        assert_true(not (target / ".proteus").exists(), "dry-run writes nothing")

        result = activate(target, dry_run=False, run_id="TEST-CODEX", aiye_home=home / "aiye")
        assert_true(result["mode"] == "activate", "activated")
        assert_true((target / ".proteus" / "activation.json").is_file(), "manifest")
        assert_true((target / ".proteus" / "ENTRY.md").is_file(), "entry")
        assert_true((target / ".proteus" / "world_env.cmd").is_file(), "world env")
        assert_true((target / "AGENTS.md").is_file(), "agents")
        agents = (target / "AGENTS.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        assert_true("BUILDER-01" in agents and "not" in agents.lower(), "rejects BUILDER-01 role")
        assert_true("Codex" in agents, "codex host named")
        assert_true("python -m proteus world resolve" in agents, "world cli")
        assert_true("{{" not in agents, "placeholders rendered")
        assert_true(not (target / ".cursor").exists(), "no .cursor tree")
        man = json.loads((target / ".proteus" / "activation.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        assert_true(man["host"] == "codex", "manifest host")
        assert_true(man["morphology"]["cursor_hooks"] is False, "no hooks claim")
        env_cmd = (target / ".proteus" / "world_env.cmd").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        expected = str((home / "aiye").resolve())
        assert_true(
            expected in env_cmd or expected.replace("\\", "/") in env_cmd.replace("\\", "/"),
            f"aiye in env; expected={expected!r} env={env_cmd!r}",
        )


def test_codex_existing_agents_preserved() -> None:
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
        home = Path(td)
        _env_isolated(home)
        target = home / "with_agents"
        target.mkdir()
        original = "# Foreign Codex Agents\nMARKER_FOREIGN_CODEX\n"
        (target / "AGENTS.md").write_text(original, encoding="utf-8")
        result = activate(target, dry_run=False, run_id="TEST-CODEX", aiye_home=home / "aiye")
        assert_true((target / "AGENTS.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8") == original, "AGENTS preserved")
        assert_true("AGENTS.md" in result["preserved_paths"], "noted preserved")
        assert_true((target / ".proteus" / "ENTRY.md").is_file(), "entry still installed")
        man = json.loads((target / ".proteus" / "activation.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        assert_true("AGENTS.md" not in man["owned_paths"], "does not own foreign AGENTS")


def test_codex_world_api_alias_and_scopes() -> None:
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
        home = Path(td)
        _env_isolated(home)
        ensure_world_home()
        store = WorldStore()
        eid = f"entity.fixture.codex.{home.name}"
        store.create_entity(
            "Fixture Router",
            entity_id=eid,
            aliases=[f"our fixture router {home.name}", f"РЅР°С€ С„РёРєСЃС‚СѓСЂРЅС‹Р№ СЂРѕСѓС‚РµСЂ {home.name}"],
        )
        resolved = resolve_entity(f"our fixture router {home.name}", world=store)
        assert_true(resolved["entity_id"] == eid, "alias resolve")
        proj_path = home / "proj"
        proj_path.mkdir()
        ent = route_observation(
            scope="ENTITY",
            text="Codex morphology test entity observation",
            observer="test_codex",
            entity_query=eid,
            project=str(proj_path),
            world=store,
        )
        assert_true(ent.get("scope") == "ENTITY", f"entity route {ent}")
        proj = route_observation(
            scope="PROJECT",
            text="Codex-only project note NEGATIVE_CONTROL_MARKER_XYZ",
            observer="test_codex",
            project=str(proj_path),
            world=store,
        )
        assert_true(proj.get("scope") == "PROJECT", f"project route {proj}")
        # Project note must not appear as entity claim text
        claims_text = " ".join(c.get("text", "") for c in store.resolve(eid)["claims"])
        assert_true("NEGATIVE_CONTROL_MARKER_XYZ" not in claims_text, "project note not entity truth")


def test_codex_uninstall() -> None:
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
        home = Path(td)
        _env_isolated(home)
        target = home / "rm_proj"
        target.mkdir()
        activate(target, dry_run=False, run_id="TEST-CODEX", aiye_home=home / "aiye")
        assert_true((target / ".proteus" / "activation.json").is_file(), "before remove")
        remove_activation(target, dry_run=False)
        assert_true(not (target / ".proteus" / "activation.json").exists(), "manifest gone")
        assert_true(not (target / "AGENTS.md").exists(), "owned agents removed")


def test_codex_refuse_cursor_manifest() -> None:
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
        home = Path(td)
        _env_isolated(home)
        target = home / "cursorish"
        target.mkdir()
        (target / ".proteus").mkdir()
        (target / ".proteus" / "activation.json").write_text(
            json.dumps({"host": "cursor", "activation_id": "x", "owned_paths": []}),
            encoding="utf-8",
        )
        try:
            activate(target, dry_run=False, aiye_home=home / "aiye")
            raise AssertionError("should refuse cursor host")
        except RuntimeError as exc:
            assert_true("not Codex" in str(exc) or "cursor" in str(exc).lower(), str(exc))


def main() -> None:
    test_codex_empty_activate()
    test_codex_existing_agents_preserved()
    test_codex_world_api_alias_and_scopes()
    test_codex_uninstall()
    test_codex_refuse_cursor_manifest()
    print("test_activate_codex: PASS")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Origin-side launcher for an independent Codex non-interactive session.

Does not resume/fork. Does not print auth tokens. Prefer --prompt-file.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import os
import re
import subprocess
import sys
import time
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path


def utc_now() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()


def scrub(text: str) -> str:
    text = re.sub(
        r"eyJ[A-Za-z0-9_\-]{10,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_\-]{10,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_\-]{10,}",
        "<JWT_REDACTED>",
        text,
    )
    text = re.sub(
        r"(sk-|key_)[A-Za-z0-9_\-]{8,}",
        "<<KEY_REDACTED>>",
        text,
        flags=re.IGNORECASE,
    )
    return text


def find_codex() -> Path:
    env = os.environ.get("CODEX_CLI")
    if env and Path(env).is_file():
        return Path(env)
    local_bin = Path(os.environ.get("LOCALAPPDATA", "")) / "OpenAI" / "Codex" / "bin"
    # Prefer versioned subdirectory CLIs when newer than the top-level shim.
    candidates: list[Path] = []
    if local_bin.is_dir():
        for p in local_bin.iterdir():
            if p.is_dir():
                exe = p / "codex.exe"
                if exe.is_file():
                    candidates.append(exe)
        top = local_bin / "codex.exe"
        if top.is_file():
            candidates.append(top)
    which = None
    try:
        import shutil

        which = shutil.which("codex")
    except Exception:
        which = None
    if which:
        candidates.append(Path(which))

    def ver_key(path: Path) -> tuple:
        try:
            out = subprocess.check_output([str(path), "--version"], text=True, errors="replace", timeout=20)
        except Exception:
            return (0, 0, 0, str(path))
        # e.g. "codex-cli 0.153.4" or "codex-cli 0.130.0-alpha.5"
        import re

        m = re.search(r"(\d+)\.(\d+)\.(\d+)", out)
        if not m:
            return (0, 0, 0, str(path))
        return (int(m.group(1)), int(m.group(2)), int(m.group(3)), str(path))

    if not candidates:
        raise SystemExit("codex executable not found; set CODEX_CLI")
    best = max(candidates, key=ver_key)
    return best


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--workspace", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--prompt", default=None)
    parser.add_argument("--prompt-file", default=None)
    parser.add_argument("--out", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--err", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--meta", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--last-message", required=True, help="codex -o last message path")
    parser.add_argument("--timeout-sec", type=int, default=0)
    parser.add_argument("--sandbox", default="danger-full-access")
    parser.add_argument("--model", default=None, help="optional -m model override")
    parser.add_argument("--ephemeral", action="store_true", default=True)
    parser.add_argument("--no-ephemeral", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--add-dir", action="append", default=[])
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if bool(args.prompt) == bool(args.prompt_file):
        raise SystemExit("Provide exactly one of --prompt or --prompt-file")
    if args.prompt_file:
        prompt = Path(args.prompt_file).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    else:
        prompt = args.prompt

    codex = find_codex()
    version = subprocess.check_output([str(codex), "--version"], text=True, errors="replace").strip()

    ephemeral = not args.no_ephemeral
    cmd = [
        str(codex),
        "exec",
        "-C",
        str(Path(args.workspace).resolve()),
        "--skip-git-repo-check",
        "-s",
        args.sandbox,
        "--dangerously-bypass-approvals-and-sandbox",
        "-c",
        "shell_environment_policy.inherit=all",
        "-o",
        str(Path(args.last_message).resolve()),
    ]
    if ephemeral:
        cmd.append("--ephemeral")
    if args.model:
        cmd.extend(["-m", args.model])
    for d in args.add_dir:
        cmd.extend(["--add-dir", d])
    # Prompt via stdin so multiline / UTF-8 is reliable; also pass `-` explicitly.
    cmd.append("-")

    env = os.environ.copy()
    # Independence: do not carry resume session ids if any ambient vars exist.
    for k in list(env):
        if "CODEX_SESSION" in k.upper() or k.upper().endswith("_RESUME"):
            env.pop(k, None)

    out_path = Path(args.out)
    err_path = Path(args.err)
    meta_path = Path(args.meta)
    out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    prompt_hash = hashlib.sha256(prompt.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
    started = utc_now()
    t0 = time.time()
    proc = subprocess.Popen(
        cmd,
        cwd=str(Path(args.workspace).resolve()),
        env=env,
        stdin=subprocess.PIPE,
        stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
        stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
    )
    try:
        stdout_b, stderr_b = proc.communicate(
            input=prompt.encode("utf-8"),
            timeout=args.timeout_sec or None,
        )
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        proc.kill()
        stdout_b, stderr_b = proc.communicate()
        timed_out = True
    else:
        timed_out = False
    elapsed = time.time() - t0
    stdout = scrub(stdout_b.decode("utf-8", errors="replace"))
    stderr = scrub(stderr_b.decode("utf-8", errors="replace"))
    out_path.write_text(stdout, encoding="utf-8")
    err_path.write_text(stderr, encoding="utf-8")

    meta = {
        "schema": "proteus.codex_launch.v0.1",
        "started_utc": started,
        "finished_utc": utc_now(),
        "elapsed_sec": elapsed,
        "timed_out": timed_out,
        "exit_code": proc.returncode,
        "pid": proc.pid,
        "codex_path": str(codex),
        "codex_version": version,
        "workspace": str(Path(args.workspace).resolve()),
        "invocation": cmd[:-1] + ["<stdin-prompt>"],
        "used_resume": False,
        "used_fork": False,
        "used_continue": False,
        "ephemeral": ephemeral,
        "sandbox": args.sandbox,
        "prompt_sha256": prompt_hash,
        "prompt_char_count": len(prompt),
        "stdout_path": str(out_path),
        "stderr_path": str(err_path),
        "last_message_path": str(Path(args.last_message).resolve()),
        "env_keys_set_for_world": sorted(
            k
            for k in ("AIYE_HOME", "PROTEUS_WORLD_ROOT", "PYTHONPATH", "PROTEUS_SOURCE_ROOT")
            if env.get(k)
        ),
    }
    meta_path.write_text(json.dumps(meta, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps({"exit_code": proc.returncode, "meta": str(meta_path), "prompt_sha256": prompt_hash}, indent=2))
    return int(proc.returncode or 0)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

# ModerBober Production Topology And Gates

## A. Server topology

- `81.26.176.248` = shared production server / SSH / debug host.
- Known project roots on this server:
  - `/opt/moderbober`
  - `/opt/quinta`
  - `/opt/whitecrow`
- Caddy/subdomain routing is shared across projects; avoid changes that can break other projects.

## B. Product URLs

- ModerBober canonical production URL:
  - `https://moderbober.mindkampf.ru/`
- Quinta:
  - `https://quinta.mindkampf.ru/`
- WhiteCrow:
  - same shared server; exact subdomain/route must be verified before using.
- Rule:
  - never treat bare IP as ModerBober product URL.

## C. Check discipline

- Browser/manual product checks:
  - use `https://moderbober.mindkampf.ru/`
- SSH/deploy/status checks:
  - use `81.26.176.248` via
  - `C:\Windows\System32\OpenSSH\ssh.exe -l deploy 81.26.176.248 ...`
- Server-local diagnostics:
  - `docker exec` / internal endpoint / `/opt/moderbober/bin/*`
- Public API checks may be auth-gated or redirected.
  - Do not treat `308` / `401` at edge as product failure without server-local verification.

## D. Manual-test gates

Manual test of real DGTU archive is blocked unless:

- production URL opens;
- Russian UI encoding is normal;
- clean test/demo session works;
- upload/intake hardening is active;
- reset/archive is active for test sessions;
- LLM status is one of:
  - `live_ok`
  - or explicit deterministic/mock mode accepted as a waived non-live test;
- opaque `provider_error` is not acceptable;
- `provider_error` must include:
  - `error_class`
  - `sanitized_error_message`
  - `http_status` if available
  - `failing_stage`
  - verification method.

## E. LLM production gate

- Runtime env file path:
  - `/opt/moderbober/secrets/llm.env`
- Backend must see:
  - `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` as boolean presence only;
  - `ANTHROPIC_MODEL` as configured model name.
- Frontend must not receive API key.
- UI badge must be clickable and show diagnostics/settings.
- `provider_error` without class/message fails the gate.
- `mock` is not an acceptable silent fallback when live provider config exists.
- Server-local diagnostics must work behind public auth gate.
- Never leak API key in git/logs/frontend/status output.

## F. Current known LLM status (as of 2026-05-27)

- Env is wired into backend runtime.
- Model is preserved from env; do not switch model automatically.
- Current live smoke result:
  - `provider_error / auth_error / HTTP 403`
- Next investigation:
  - Anthropic auth/access/request issue; do not start with model switching by default.
# ModerBober Moderator Cockpit Interface Law v0.1

Р“Р»Р°РІРЅС‹Р№ РѕР±СЉРµРєС‚ РїСЂРѕРґСѓРєС‚Р° вЂ” РёР·РјРµРЅСЏСЋС‰РµРµСЃСЏ СЃРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏРЅРёРµ РіСЂСѓРїРїС‹:

- РѕР±СЉРµРєС‚ СЂР°Р±РѕС‚С‹;
- С‚РµРјР°;
- РїСЂРѕР±Р»РµРјР°;
- СЃРёС‚СѓР°С†РёСЏ;
- С†РµР»СЊ;
- РїСЂРѕРµРєС‚РЅРѕРµ СЂРµС€РµРЅРёРµ;

- РїРѕР·РёС†РёРё СѓС‡Р°СЃС‚РЅРёРєРѕРІ;
- СЃР°РјРѕРѕРїСЂРµРґРµР»РµРЅРёРµ;
- РіСЂСѓРїРїРѕРІР°СЏ РґРёРЅР°РјРёРєР°;
- Р»РёРЅРёСЏ РґРѕРєР»Р°РґРѕРІ;

- РґРІРёР¶РµРЅРёРµ РїРѕ Р°СЂРєРµ СЃРµСЃСЃРёРё.

РћРЅ РїРѕС…РѕР¶ РЅРµ РЅР° С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†Сѓ Рё РЅРµ РЅР° dashboard, Р° РЅР° РєР°СЂС‚Сѓ РІСЂРѕРґРµ Osterwalder canvas: РЅР°Р±РѕСЂ РїРѕР»РµР№/РєР°СЂС‚РѕС‡РµРє, РјРµР¶РґСѓ РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹РјРё РІРёРґРЅС‹ СЃРІСЏР·Рё.

codex
I found a key handoff risk in the target: its roadmap documents contain older milestone narratives alongside a newer v2.9.4 focus, while the actual working tree is dirty. The capsule will preserve those as sourced, potentially conflicting signals instead of inventing a single “current” story.

+"""Create a compact, source-grounded handoff capsule for a coding project.
+
+The capsule deliberately collects only durable entry material and observable git
+state.  It is not an archive and it does not attempt to infer a task from an
+agent transcript.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import re
+import subprocess
+from dataclasses import dataclass
+from datetime import datetime, timezone
+from pathlib import Path
+
+
+DEFAULT_MAX_CHARS = 48_000
+IGNORED_DIRS = {".git", ".next", "node_modules", ".venv", "__pycache__"}
+
+# Ordered from mandatory project instructions through likely current-work
+# carriers.  A missing file is normal; projects vary widely.
+DOCUMENT_CANDIDATES = (
+    ("Project instructions", "AGENTS.md", 5_000),
+    ("Project instructions", "CLAUDE.md", 3_000),
+    ("Project overview", "README.md", 5_000),
+    ("Current-state carrier", "CURRENT.md", 5_000),
+    ("Current-state carrier", "FRONTIER.md", 5_000),
+    ("Current-state carrier", "PROJECT_INTENT_AND_CURRENT_STATE.md", 8_000),
+    ("Roadmap / next work", "ROADMAP_NEXT_STEPS.md", 8_000),
+    ("Handoff", "HANDOFF.md", 5_000),
+    ("Contribution guide", "CONTRIBUTING.md", 4_000),
+    ("Operations constraints", "docs/ops/PRODUCTION_TOPOLOGY_AND_GATES.md", 6_000),
+    ("Product law", "docs/product/MODERATOR_COCKPIT_INTERFACE_LAW_v0_1.md", 7_000),
+)
+MANIFEST_CANDIDATES = (
+    "pyproject.toml",
+    "package.json",
+    "requirements.txt",
+    "backend/requirements.txt",
+    "frontend/package.json",
+    "docker-compose.yml",
+    "docker-compose.yaml",
+    "Makefile",
+)
+
+
+def _run(project: Path, *args: str) -> tuple[int, str]:
+    try:
+        completed = subprocess.run(
+            args,
+            cwd=project,
+            capture_output=True,
+            text=True,
+            encoding="utf-8",
+            errors="replace",
+            timeout=20,
+        )
+    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as exc:
+        return 1, str(exc)
+    return completed.returncode, completed.stdout.strip()
+
+
+def _redact(text: str) -> str:
+    """Remove common credential forms before a capsule is written."""
+    patterns = (
+        r"(?i)(api[_-]?key|token|password|secret)\s*([=:])\s*[^\s'\"`]+",
+        r"\bsk-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{12,}\b",
+        r"\beyJ[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}\b",
+    )
+    for pattern in patterns:
+        text = re.sub(pattern, "<REDACTED>", text)
+    return text
+
+
+def _excerpt(path: Path, limit: int) -> str:
+    try:
+        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
+    except OSError as exc:
+        return f"[Could not read: {exc}]"
+    text = _redact(text).strip()
+    if len(text) <= limit:
+        return text
+    # Keep the beginning, which normally contains scope and instructions, and
+    # label the loss so a receiver does not mistake it for a complete document.
+    return text[:limit].rstrip() + f"\n\n[Excerpt truncated at {limit:,} characters; inspect {path.name} before relying on omitted detail.]"
+
+
+def _count_files(project: Path) -> int:
+    count = 0
+    for path in project.rglob("*"):
+        if any(part in IGNORED_DIRS for part in path.parts):
+            continue
+        if path.is_file():
+            count += 1
+    return count
+
+
+def _git_state(project: Path) -> tuple[list[str], str, str]:
+    code, root = _run(project, "git", "rev-parse", "--show-toplevel")
+    if code:
+        return [], "Not a Git repository (or git unavailable).", ""
+    _, branch = _run(project, "git", "branch", "--show-current")
+    _, head = _run(project, "git", "log", "-1", "--format=%H")
+    _, log = _run(project, "git", "log", "-8", "--date=short", "--format=%h %ad %s")
+    _, porcelain = _run(project, "git", "status", "--porcelain=v1", "-uall")
+    changed = porcelain.splitlines() if porcelain else []
+    status = [
+        f"Git root: `{root}`",
+        f"Branch: `{branch or '(detached HEAD)'}`",
+        f"HEAD: `{head or '(unavailable)'}`",
+        f"Working tree: {'clean' if not changed else f'dirty ({len(changed)} paths)'}",
+    ]
+    if changed:
+        shown = changed[:80]
+        status.append("Changed paths (porcelain; inspect before editing):\n```text\n" + "\n".join(shown) + "\n```")
+        if len(changed) > len(shown):
+            status.append(f"[Only first {len(shown)} of {len(changed)} changed paths shown.]")
+    return status, log or "(No commits found.)", branch
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class CapsuleResult:
+    project: Path
+    output: Path
+    chars: int
+    included_documents: tuple[str, ...]
+
+
+def build_capsule(project: Path, output: Path, *, max_chars: int = DEFAULT_MAX_CHARS) -> CapsuleResult:
+    """Write one Markdown capsule.  Does not mutate *project*."""
+    project = project.expanduser().resolve()
+    output = output.expanduser().resolve()
+    if not project.is_dir():
+        raise ValueError(f"project is not a directory: {project}")
+    if max_chars < 4_000:
+        raise ValueError("max_chars must be at least 4000")
+
+    git_state, recent_log, branch = _git_state(project)
+    top_level = sorted(p.name + ("/" if p.is_dir() else "") for p in project.iterdir() if p.name not in IGNORED_DIRS)
+    lines = [
+        "# Project Capsule",
+        "",
+        "> Generated context, not a source of truth. Verify cited files and current Git state before changing the project.",
+        "",
+        "## Receiver protocol",
+        "",
+        "1. Treat the project instructions and constraints below as binding.",
+        "2. Preserve existing dirty work: inspect it before editing or cleaning anything.",
+        "3. Reconcile any conflicting roadmap/current-state material by checking Git history and the cited source files.",
+        "4. Before implementation, read the listed files most relevant to the chosen change and run the narrowest documented check.",
+        "",
+        "## Observed repository",
+        "",
+        f"- Project: `{project}`",
+        f"- Generated (UTC): `{datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()}`",
+        f"- Approximate tracked/working source files (excluding common dependency/cache directories): {_count_files(project):,}",
+        f"- Top level: {', '.join(f'`{name}`' for name in top_level[:60])}" + (" …" if len(top_level) > 60 else ""),
+        "",
+        "## Git working situation",
+        "",
+        *git_state,
+        "",
+        "### Recent commits",
+        "",
+        "```text",
+        recent_log,
+        "```",
+        "",
+        "## Runtime / build manifests",
+        "",
+    ]
+    for rel in MANIFEST_CANDIDATES:
+        path = project / rel
+        if path.is_file():
+            lines.extend((f"### `{rel}`", "", "```text", _excerpt(path, 3_000), "```", ""))
+
+    included: list[str] = []
+    # Reserve room for headings and the source map. Individual excerpts are
+    # additionally bounded; the global check below never silently overflows.
+    for category, rel, limit in DOCUMENT_CANDIDATES:
+        path = project / rel
+        if not path.is_file():
+            continue
+        remaining = max_chars - len("\n".join(lines)) - 1_600
+        if remaining < 500:
+            break
+        excerpt = _excerpt(path, min(limit, max(500, remaining)))
+        lines.extend((f"## {category}: `{rel}`", "", excerpt, ""))
+        included.append(rel)
+
+    lines.extend((
+        "## What to inspect before changing code",
+        "",
+        "- The mandatory instruction files above, plus any documents named by them for the surface you will change.",
+        "- The current diff for every dirty path that overlaps your task; do not assume it is disposable.",
+        "- The owning backend/frontend module and its nearest existing smoke or test script.",
+        "- Deployment/production gates before touching deploy, Caddy, provider configuration, or a manual-test gate.",
+        "",
+        "## Capsule boundaries",
+        "",
+        "- This is a compact index and excerpts, not an archive, a transcript, or a substitute for source inspection.",
+        "- It intentionally omits untracked file contents, Git diff bodies, binaries, dependency trees, and environment files.",
+        "- Common credential-shaped values are redacted heuristically; never treat a capsule as a safe place for secrets.",
+        "- `current` and `next` claims remain attributed to the included documents and Git observation; documents can be stale or conflict.",
+        "",
+        "## Included source map",
+        "",
+        *[f"- `{item}`" for item in included],
+    ))
+    text = "\n".join(lines).rstrip() + "\n"
+    if len(text) > max_chars:
+        # This should be rare because documents are budgeted. Preserve a valid,
+        # explicitly incomplete result instead of failing after expensive scan.
+        text = text[: max_chars - 120].rstrip() + "\n\n[Capsule reached global size limit; inspect project files directly.]\n"
+    output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    output.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
+    return CapsuleResult(project=project, output=output, chars=len(text), included_documents=tuple(included))

+"""Create a compact, source-grounded handoff capsule for a coding project.
+
+The capsule deliberately collects only durable entry material and observable git
+state.  It is not an archive and it does not attempt to infer a task from an
+agent transcript.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import re
+import subprocess
+from dataclasses import dataclass
+from datetime import datetime, timezone
+from pathlib import Path
+
+
+DEFAULT_MAX_CHARS = 48_000
+IGNORED_DIRS = {".git", ".next", "node_modules", ".venv", "__pycache__"}
+
+# Ordered from mandatory project instructions through likely current-work
+# carriers.  A missing file is normal; projects vary widely.
+DOCUMENT_CANDIDATES = (
+    ("Project instructions", "AGENTS.md", 5_000),
+    ("Project instructions", "CLAUDE.md", 3_000),
+    ("Project overview", "README.md", 5_000),
+    ("Current-state carrier", "CURRENT.md", 5_000),
+    ("Current-state carrier", "FRONTIER.md", 5_000),
+    ("Current-state carrier", "PROJECT_INTENT_AND_CURRENT_STATE.md", 8_000),
+    ("Roadmap / next work", "ROADMAP_NEXT_STEPS.md", 8_000),
+    ("Handoff", "HANDOFF.md", 5_000),
+    ("Contribution guide", "CONTRIBUTING.md", 4_000),
+    ("Operations constraints", "docs/ops/PRODUCTION_TOPOLOGY_AND_GATES.md", 6_000),
+    ("Product law", "docs/product/MODERATOR_COCKPIT_INTERFACE_LAW_v0_1.md", 7_000),
+)
+MANIFEST_CANDIDATES = (
+    "pyproject.toml",
+    "package.json",
+    "requirements.txt",
+    "backend/requirements.txt",
+    "frontend/package.json",
+    "docker-compose.yml",
+    "docker-compose.yaml",
+    "Makefile",
+)
+
+
+def _run(project: Path, *args: str) -> tuple[int, str]:
+    try:
+        completed = subprocess.run(
+            args,
+            cwd=project,
+            capture_output=True,
+            text=True,
+            encoding="utf-8",
+            errors="replace",
+            timeout=20,
+        )
+    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as exc:
+        return 1, str(exc)
+    return completed.returncode, completed.stdout.strip()
+
+
+def _redact(text: str) -> str:
+    """Remove common credential forms before a capsule is written."""
+    patterns = (
+        r"(?i)(api[_-]?key|token|password|secret)\s*([=:])\s*[^\s'\"`]+",
+        r"\bsk-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{12,}\b",
+        r"\beyJ[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}\b",
+    )
+    for pattern in patterns:
+        text = re.sub(pattern, "<REDACTED>", text)
+    return text
+
+
+def _excerpt(path: Path, limit: int) -> str:
+    try:
+        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
+    except OSError as exc:
+        return f"[Could not read: {exc}]"
+    text = _redact(text).strip()
+    if len(text) <= limit:
+        return text
+    # Keep the beginning, which normally contains scope and instructions, and
+    # label the loss so a receiver does not mistake it for a complete document.
+    return text[:limit].rstrip() + f"\n\n[Excerpt truncated at {limit:,} characters; inspect {path.name} before relying on omitted detail.]"
+
+
+def _count_files(project: Path) -> int:
+    count = 0
+    for path in project.rglob("*"):
+        if any(part in IGNORED_DIRS for part in path.parts):
+            continue
+        if path.is_file():
+            count += 1
+    return count
+
+
+def _git_state(project: Path) -> tuple[list[str], str, str]:
+    code, root = _run(project, "git", "rev-parse", "--show-toplevel")
+    if code:
+        return [], "Not a Git repository (or git unavailable).", ""
+    _, branch = _run(project, "git", "branch", "--show-current")
+    _, head = _run(project, "git", "log", "-1", "--format=%H")
+    _, log = _run(project, "git", "log", "-8", "--date=short", "--format=%h %ad %s")
+    _, porcelain = _run(project, "git", "status", "--porcelain=v1", "-uall")
+    changed = porcelain.splitlines() if porcelain else []
+    status = [
+        f"Git root: `{root}`",
+        f"Branch: `{branch or '(detached HEAD)'}`",
+        f"HEAD: `{head or '(unavailable)'}`",
+        f"Working tree: {'clean' if not changed else f'dirty ({len(changed)} paths)'}",
+    ]
+    if changed:
+        shown = changed[:80]
+        status.append("Changed paths (porcelain; inspect before editing):\n```text\n" + "\n".join(shown) + "\n```")
+        if len(changed) > len(shown):
+            status.append(f"[Only first {len(shown)} of {len(changed)} changed paths shown.]")
+    return status, log or "(No commits found.)", branch
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class CapsuleResult:
+    project: Path
+    output: Path
+    chars: int
+    included_documents: tuple[str, ...]
+
+
+def build_capsule(project: Path, output: Path, *, max_chars: int = DEFAULT_MAX_CHARS) -> CapsuleResult:
+    """Write one Markdown capsule.  Does not mutate *project*."""
+    project = project.expanduser().resolve()
+    output = output.expanduser().resolve()
+    if not project.is_dir():

+    if max_chars < 4_000:
+        raise ValueError("max_chars must be at least 4000")
+

+        "",

+        "- The mandatory instruction files above, plus any documents named by them for the surface you will change.",
+        "- The current diff for every dirty path that overlaps your task; do not assume it is disposable.",
+        "- The owning backend/frontend module and its nearest existing smoke or test script.",
+        "- Deployment/production gates before touching deploy, Caddy, provider configuration, or a manual-test gate.",
+        "",

+        "",

 (`PYTHONPATH=src` if needed.) Writes Proteus-owned surfaces and `.proteus/ENTRY.md`; does not overwrite an existing `AGENTS.md` or non-`proteus-*` Cursor rules. Not Gate 1.
+
+Project capsule (compact, practical context for a fresh coding agent):
+
+```
+python -m proteus capsule --project C:\path\to\project --out C:\handoff\PROJECT_CAPSULE.md
+```
+
+The command is read-only with respect to the selected project. It records observed Git state, manifests, mandatory instructions, and bounded excerpts from standard overview/current-state/roadmap documents. Give the Markdown file to the fresh agent as project context, then have it inspect the cited sources before editing. It deliberately omits diff bodies, untracked file contents, binaries, dependency trees, environment files, and agent transcripts; credential-shaped strings in included excerpts are heuristically redacted. Use `--max-chars` (default 48,000; minimum 4,000) to bound the result.

+        path = project / rel
+        if path.is_file():
+            lines.extend((f"### `{rel}`", "", "```text", _excerpt(path, 3_000), "```", ""))
+
+    included: list[str] = []
+    # Reserve room for headings and the source map. Individual excerpts are
+    # additionally bounded; the global check below never silently overflows.
+    for category, rel, limit in DOCUMENT_CANDIDATES:
+        path = project / rel
+        if not path.is_file():
+            continue
+        remaining = max_chars - len("\n".join(lines)) - 1_600
+        if remaining < 500:
+            break
+        excerpt = _excerpt(path, min(limit, max(500, remaining)))
+        lines.extend((f"## {category}: `{rel}`", "", excerpt, ""))
+        included.append(rel)
+
+    lines.extend((
+        "## What to inspect before changing code",
+        "",
+        "- The mandatory instruction files above, plus any documents named by them for the surface you will change.",
+        "- The current diff for every dirty path that overlaps your task; do not assume it is disposable.",
+        "- The owning backend/frontend module and its nearest existing smoke or test script.",
+        "- Deployment/production gates before touching deploy, Caddy, provider configuration, or a manual-test gate.",
+        "",
+        "## Capsule boundaries",
+        "",
+        "- This is a compact index and excerpts, not an archive, a transcript, or a substitute for source inspection.",
+        "- It intentionally omits untracked file contents, Git diff bodies, binaries, dependency trees, and environment files.",
+        "- Common credential-shaped values are redacted heuristically; never treat a capsule as a safe place for secrets.",
+        "- `current` and `next` claims remain attributed to the included documents and Git observation; documents can be stale or conflict.",
+        "",
+        "## Included source map",
+        "",
+        *[f"- `{item}`" for item in included],
+    ))
+    text = "\n".join(lines).rstrip() + "\n"
+    if len(text) > max_chars:
+        # This should be rare because documents are budgeted. Preserve a valid,
+        # explicitly incomplete result instead of failing after expensive scan.
+        text = text[: max_chars - 120].rstrip() + "\n\n[Capsule reached global size limit; inspect project files directly.]\n"
+    output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    output.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
+    return CapsuleResult(project=project, output=output, chars=len(text), included_documents=tuple(included))

+"""Tests for compact, source-grounded project handoff capsules."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import os
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "src"))
+
+from proteus.capsule import build_capsule  # noqa: E402
+
+
+def run(project: Path, *args: str) -> None:
+    subprocess.run(args, cwd=project, check=True, capture_output=True, text=True)
+
+
+def test_capsule_contains_handoff_essentials_and_redacts() -> None:
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+        root = Path(td)
+        project = root / "project"
+        project.mkdir()
+        (project / "AGENTS.md").write_text("# Rules\nRead docs/law.md first.\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        (project / "README.md").write_text("# Example\nA practical application.\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        (project / "PROJECT_INTENT_AND_CURRENT_STATE.md").write_text(
+            "# Current\nThe active focus is migration safety.\nAPI_KEY=should_not_escape\n",
+            encoding="utf-8",
+        )
+        (project / "ROADMAP_NEXT_STEPS.md").write_text(
+            "# Next\nRun the targeted migration smoke before a broad suite.\n",
+            encoding="utf-8",
+        )
+        (project / "package.json").write_text('{"name":"example"}\n', encoding="utf-8")
+        (project / "changed.py").write_text("before\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        run(project, "git", "init")
+        run(project, "git", "config", "user.email", "test@example.invalid")
+        run(project, "git", "config", "user.name", "Capsule Test")
+        run(project, "git", "add", ".")
+        run(project, "git", "commit", "-m", "initial")
+        (project / "changed.py").write_text("after\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        (project / "untracked.txt").write_text("local work\n", encoding="utf-8")
+
+        out = root / "capsule.md"
+        result = build_capsule(project, out, max_chars=12_000)
+        text = out.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+        assert result.output == out.resolve()
+        assert result.chars == len(text)
+        assert "Read docs/law.md first" in text
+        assert "active focus is migration safety" in text
+        assert "targeted migration smoke" in text
+        assert "Working tree: dirty (2 paths)" in text
+        assert "changed.py" in text and "untracked.txt" in text
+        assert "should_not_escape" not in text
+        assert "<REDACTED>" in text
+        assert "What to inspect before changing code" in text
+
+
+def test_capsule_respects_global_bound_and_does_not_mutate_project() -> None:
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+        root = Path(td)
+        project = root / "project"
+        project.mkdir()
+        original = "# Readme\n" + ("useful context\n" * 2_000)
+        (project / "README.md").write_text(original, encoding="utf-8")
+        out = root / "capsule.md"
+        result = build_capsule(project, out, max_chars=4_500)
+        assert result.chars <= 4_500
+        assert (project / "README.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8") == original
+        assert "Generated context, not a source of truth" in out.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+
+
+def main() -> None:
+    test_capsule_contains_handoff_essentials_and_redacts()
+    test_capsule_respects_global_bound_and_does_not_mutate_project()
+    print("test_capsule: PASS")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()

 ```
 
 (`PYTHONPATH=src` if needed.) Writes Proteus-owned surfaces and `.proteus/ENTRY.md`; does not overwrite an existing `AGENTS.md` or non-`proteus-*` Cursor rules. Not Gate 1.
+
+Project capsule (compact, practical context for a fresh coding agent):
+
+```
+python -m proteus capsule --project C:\path\to\project --out C:\handoff\PROJECT_CAPSULE.md
+```
+
+The command is read-only with respect to the selected project. It records observed Git state, manifests, mandatory instructions, and bounded excerpts from standard overview/current-state/roadmap documents. Give the Markdown file to the fresh agent as project context, then have it inspect the cited sources before editing. It deliberately omits diff bodies, untracked file contents, binaries, dependency trees, environment files, and agent transcripts; credential-shaped strings in included excerpts are heuristically redacted. Use `--max-chars` (default 48,000; minimum 4,000) to bound the result.

+"""Create a compact, source-grounded handoff capsule for a coding project.
+
+The capsule deliberately collects only durable entry material and observable git
+state.  It is not an archive and it does not attempt to infer a task from an
+agent transcript.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import re
+import subprocess
+from dataclasses import dataclass
+from datetime import datetime, timezone
+from pathlib import Path
+
+
+DEFAULT_MAX_CHARS = 48_000
+IGNORED_DIRS = {".git", ".next", "node_modules", ".venv", "__pycache__"}
+
+# Ordered from mandatory project instructions through likely current-work
+# carriers.  A missing file is normal; projects vary widely.
+DOCUMENT_CANDIDATES = (
+    ("Project instructions", "AGENTS.md", 5_000),
+    ("Project instructions", "CLAUDE.md", 3_000),
+    ("Project overview", "README.md", 5_000),
+    ("Current-state carrier", "CURRENT.md", 5_000),
+    ("Current-state carrier", "FRONTIER.md", 5_000),
+    ("Current-state carrier", "PROJECT_INTENT_AND_CURRENT_STATE.md", 8_000),
+    ("Roadmap / next work", "ROADMAP_NEXT_STEPS.md", 8_000),
+    ("Handoff", "HANDOFF.md", 5_000),
+    ("Contribution guide", "CONTRIBUTING.md", 4_000),
+    ("Operations constraints", "docs/ops/PRODUCTION_TOPOLOGY_AND_GATES.md", 6_000),
+    ("Product law", "docs/product/MODERATOR_COCKPIT_INTERFACE_LAW_v0_1.md", 7_000),
+)
+MANIFEST_CANDIDATES = (
+    "pyproject.toml",
+    "package.json",
+    "requirements.txt",
+    "backend/requirements.txt",
+    "frontend/package.json",
+    "docker-compose.yml",
+    "docker-compose.yaml",
+    "Makefile",
+)
+
+
+def _run(project: Path, *args: str) -> tuple[int, str]:
+    try:
+        completed = subprocess.run(
+            args,
+            cwd=project,
+            capture_output=True,
+            text=True,
+            encoding="utf-8",
+            errors="replace",
+            timeout=20,
+        )
+    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as exc:
+        return 1, str(exc)
+    return completed.returncode, completed.stdout.strip()
+
+
+def _redact(text: str) -> str:
+    """Remove common credential forms before a capsule is written."""
+    patterns = (
+        r"(?i)(api[_-]?key|token|password|secret)\s*([=:])\s*[^\s'\"`]+",
+        r"\bsk-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{12,}\b",
+        r"\beyJ[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}\b",
+    )
+    for pattern in patterns:
+        text = re.sub(pattern, "<REDACTED>", text)
+    return text
+
+
+def _excerpt(path: Path, limit: int) -> str:
+    try:
+        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
+    except OSError as exc:
+        return f"[Could not read: {exc}]"
+    text = _redact(text).strip()
+    if len(text) <= limit:
+        return text
+    # Keep the beginning, which normally contains scope and instructions, and
+    # label the loss so a receiver does not mistake it for a complete document.
+    return text[:limit].rstrip() + f"\n\n[Excerpt truncated at {limit:,} characters; inspect {path.name} before relying on omitted detail.]"
+
+
+def _count_files(project: Path) -> int:
+    count = 0
+    for path in project.rglob("*"):
+        if any(part in IGNORED_DIRS for part in path.parts):
+            continue
+        if path.is_file():
+            count += 1
+    return count
+
+
+def _git_state(project: Path) -> tuple[list[str], str, str]:
+    code, root = _run(project, "git", "rev-parse", "--show-toplevel")
+    if code:
+        return [], "Not a Git repository (or git unavailable).", ""
+    _, branch = _run(project, "git", "branch", "--show-current")
+    _, head = _run(project, "git", "log", "-1", "--format=%H")
+    _, log = _run(project, "git", "log", "-8", "--date=short", "--format=%h %ad %s")
+    _, porcelain = _run(project, "git", "status", "--porcelain=v1", "-uall")
+    changed = porcelain.splitlines() if porcelain else []
+    status = [
+        f"Git root: `{root}`",
+        f"Branch: `{branch or '(detached HEAD)'}`",
+        f"HEAD: `{head or '(unavailable)'}`",
+        f"Working tree: {'clean' if not changed else f'dirty ({len(changed)} paths)'}",
+    ]
+    if changed:
+        shown = changed[:80]
+        status.append("Changed paths (porcelain; inspect before editing):\n```text\n" + "\n".join(shown) + "\n```")
+        if len(changed) > len(shown):
+            status.append(f"[Only first {len(shown)} of {len(changed)} changed paths shown.]")
+    return status, log or "(No commits found.)", branch
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class CapsuleResult:
+    project: Path
+    output: Path
+    chars: int
+    included_documents: tuple[str, ...]
+
+
+def build_capsule(project: Path, output: Path, *, max_chars: int = DEFAULT_MAX_CHARS) -> CapsuleResult:
+    """Write one Markdown capsule.  Does not mutate *project*."""
+    project = project.expanduser().resolve()
+    output = output.expanduser().resolve()
+    if not project.is_dir():
+        raise ValueError(f"project is not a directory: {project}")
+    if max_chars < 4_000:
+        raise ValueError("max_chars must be at least 4000")
+
+    git_state, recent_log, branch = _git_state(project)
+    top_level = sorted(p.name + ("/" if p.is_dir() else "") for p in project.iterdir() if p.name not in IGNORED_DIRS)
+    lines = [
+        "# Project Capsule",
+        "",
+        "> Generated context, not a source of truth. Verify cited files and current Git state before changing the project.",
+        "",
+        "## Receiver protocol",
+        "",
+        "1. Treat the project instructions and constraints below as binding.",
+        "2. Preserve existing dirty work: inspect it before editing or cleaning anything.",
+        "3. Reconcile any conflicting roadmap/current-state material by checking Git history and the cited source files.",
+        "4. Before implementation, read the listed files most relevant to the chosen change and run the narrowest documented check.",
+        "",
+        "## Observed repository",
+        "",
+        f"- Project: `{project}`",
+        f"- Generated (UTC): `{datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()}`",
+        f"- Approximate tracked/working source files (excluding common dependency/cache directories): {_count_files(project):,}",
+        f"- Top level: {', '.join(f'`{name}`' for name in top_level[:60])}" + (" …" if len(top_level) > 60 else ""),
+        "",
+        "## Git working situation",
+        "",
+        *git_state,
+        "",
+        "### Recent commits",
+        "",
+        "```text",
+        recent_log,
+        "```",
+        "",
+        "## Runtime / build manifests",
+        "",
+    ]
+    for rel in MANIFEST_CANDIDATES:
+        path = project / rel
+        if path.is_file():
+            lines.extend((f"### `{rel}`", "", "```text", _excerpt(path, 3_000), "```", ""))
+
+    included: list[str] = []
+    # Reserve room for headings and the source map. Individual excerpts are
+    # additionally bounded; the global check below never silently overflows.
+    for category, rel, limit in DOCUMENT_CANDIDATES:
+        path = project / rel
+        if not path.is_file():
+            continue
+        remaining = max_chars - len("\n".join(lines)) - 1_600
+        if remaining < 500:
+            break
+        excerpt = _excerpt(path, min(limit, max(500, remaining)))
+        lines.extend((f"## {category}: `{rel}`", "", excerpt, ""))
+        included.append(rel)
+
+    lines.extend((
+        "## What to inspect before changing code",
+        "",
+        "- The mandatory instruction files above, plus any documents named by them for the surface you will change.",
+        "- The current diff for every dirty path that overlaps your task; do not assume it is disposable.",
+        "- The owning backend/frontend module and its nearest existing smoke or test script.",
+        "- Deployment/production gates before touching deploy, Caddy, provider configuration, or a manual-test gate.",
+        "",
+        "## Capsule boundaries",
+        "",
+        "- This is a compact index and excerpts, not an archive, a transcript, or a substitute for source inspection.",
+        "- It intentionally omits untracked file contents, Git diff bodies, binaries, dependency trees, and environment files.",
+        "- Common credential-shaped values are redacted heuristically; never treat a capsule as a safe place for secrets.",
+        "- `current` and `next` claims remain attributed to the included documents and Git observation; documents can be stale or conflict.",
+        "",
+        "## Included source map",
+        "",
+        *[f"- `{item}`" for item in included],
+    ))
+    text = "\n".join(lines).rstrip() + "\n"
+    if len(text) > max_chars:
+        # This should be rare because documents are budgeted. Preserve a valid,
+        # explicitly incomplete result instead of failing after expensive scan.
+        text = text[: max_chars - 120].rstrip() + "\n\n[Capsule reached global size limit; inspect project files directly.]\n"
+    output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    output.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
+    return CapsuleResult(project=project, output=output, chars=len(text), included_documents=tuple(included))

+    test_capsule_respects_global_bound_and_does_not_mutate_project()
+    print("test_capsule: PASS")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()

 (`PYTHONPATH=src` if needed.) Writes Proteus-owned surfaces and `.proteus/ENTRY.md`; does not overwrite an existing `AGENTS.md` or non-`proteus-*` Cursor rules. Not Gate 1.
+
+Project capsule (compact, practical context for a fresh coding agent):
+
+```
+python -m proteus capsule --project C:\path\to\project --out C:\handoff\PROJECT_CAPSULE.md
+```
+
+The command is read-only with respect to the selected project. It records observed Git state, manifests, mandatory instructions, and bounded excerpts from standard overview/current-state/roadmap documents. Give the Markdown file to the fresh agent as project context, then have it inspect the cited sources before editing. It deliberately omits diff bodies, untracked file contents, binaries, dependency trees, environment files, and agent transcripts; credential-shaped strings in included excerpts are heuristically redacted. Use `--max-chars` (default 48,000; minimum 4,000) to bound the result.
