+"""Tests for compact, source-grounded project handoff capsules."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import os
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "src"))
+
+from proteus.capsule import build_capsule  # noqa: E402
+
+
+def run(project: Path, *args: str) -> None:
+    subprocess.run(args, cwd=project, check=True, capture_output=True, text=True)
+
+
+def test_capsule_contains_handoff_essentials_and_redacts() -> None:
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+        root = Path(td)
+        project = root / "project"
+        project.mkdir()
+        (project / "AGENTS.md").write_text("# Rules\nRead docs/law.md first.\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        (project / "README.md").write_text("# Example\nA practical application.\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        (project / "PROJECT_INTENT_AND_CURRENT_STATE.md").write_text(
+            "# Current\nThe active focus is migration safety.\nAPI_KEY=should_not_escape\n",
+            encoding="utf-8",
+        )
+        (project / "ROADMAP_NEXT_STEPS.md").write_text(
+            "# Next\nRun the targeted migration smoke before a broad suite.\n",
+            encoding="utf-8",
+        )
+        (project / "package.json").write_text('{"name":"example"}\n', encoding="utf-8")
+        (project / "changed.py").write_text("before\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        run(project, "git", "init")
+        run(project, "git", "config", "user.email", "test@example.invalid")
+        run(project, "git", "config", "user.name", "Capsule Test")
+        run(project, "git", "add", ".")
+        run(project, "git", "commit", "-m", "initial")
+        (project / "changed.py").write_text("after\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        (project / "untracked.txt").write_text("local work\n", encoding="utf-8")
+
+        out = root / "capsule.md"
+        result = build_capsule(project, out, max_chars=12_000)
+        text = out.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+        assert result.output == out.resolve()
+        assert result.chars == len(text)
+        assert "Read docs/law.md first" in text
+        assert "active focus is migration safety" in text
+        assert "targeted migration smoke" in text
+        assert "Working tree: dirty (2 paths)" in text
+        assert "Changed-path triage: 2 non-cache-shaped paths" in text
+        assert "changed.py" in text and "untracked.txt" in text
+        assert "should_not_escape" not in text
+        assert "<REDACTED>" in text
+        assert "What to inspect before changing code" in text
+
+
+def test_capsule_respects_global_bound_and_does_not_mutate_project() -> None:
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+        root = Path(td)
+        project = root / "project"
+        project.mkdir()
+        original = "# Readme\n" + ("useful context\n" * 2_000)
+        (project / "README.md").write_text(original, encoding="utf-8")

+def test_capsule_summarizes_cache_shaped_dirty_paths() -> None:

+        root = Path(td)
+        project = root / "project"
+        cache = project / "SAS" / ".edge-render-x" / "Default" / "Cache"
+        cache.mkdir(parents=True)
+        (project / "README.md").write_text("# Example\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        (project / "useful.txt").write_text("keep me\n", encoding="utf-8")

+        assert "useful.txt" in text

+    print("test_capsule: PASS")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()

 ```
 
 (`PYTHONPATH=src` if needed.) Writes Proteus-owned surfaces and `.proteus/ENTRY.md`; does not overwrite an existing `AGENTS.md` or non-`proteus-*` Cursor rules. Not Gate 1.
+
+Project capsule (compact, practical context for a fresh coding agent):
+
+```
+python -m proteus capsule --project C:\path\to\project --out C:\handoff\PROJECT_CAPSULE.md
+```
+
+The command is read-only with respect to the selected project. It records observed Git state, manifests, mandatory instructions, and bounded excerpts from standard overview/current-state/roadmap documents. Give the Markdown file to the fresh agent as project context, then have it inspect the cited sources before editing. It deliberately omits diff bodies, untracked file contents, binaries, dependency trees, environment files, and agent transcripts; credential-shaped strings in included excerpts are heuristically redacted. Use `--max-chars` (default 48,000; minimum 4,000) to bound the result.

+"""Create a compact, source-grounded handoff capsule for a coding project.
+
+The capsule deliberately collects only durable entry material and observable git
+state.  It is not an archive and it does not attempt to infer a task from an
+agent transcript.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import re
+import subprocess
+from dataclasses import dataclass
+from datetime import datetime, timezone
+from pathlib import Path
+
+
+DEFAULT_MAX_CHARS = 48_000
+IGNORED_DIRS = {".git", ".next", "node_modules", ".venv", "__pycache__"}
+
+# Ordered from mandatory project instructions through likely current-work
+# carriers.  A missing file is normal; projects vary widely.
+DOCUMENT_CANDIDATES = (
+    ("Project instructions", "AGENTS.md", 3_000),
+    ("Project instructions", "CLAUDE.md", 3_000),
+    ("Operations constraints", "docs/ops/PRODUCTION_TOPOLOGY_AND_GATES.md", 4_500),
+    ("Product law", "docs/product/MODERATOR_COCKPIT_INTERFACE_LAW_v0_1.md", 5_000),
+    ("Project overview", "README.md", 3_000),
+    ("Current-state carrier", "CURRENT.md", 5_000),
+    ("Current-state carrier", "FRONTIER.md", 5_000),
+    ("Current-state carrier", "PROJECT_INTENT_AND_CURRENT_STATE.md", 7_000),
+    ("Current plan referenced by roadmap", "RECOVERY_REENTRY_MASTER_PLAN_v2_3_to_v3_4.md", 6_000),
+    ("Roadmap / next work", "ROADMAP_NEXT_STEPS.md", 5_000),
+    ("Handoff", "HANDOFF.md", 5_000),
+    ("Contribution guide", "CONTRIBUTING.md", 4_000),
+)
+MANIFEST_CANDIDATES = (
+    "pyproject.toml",
+    "package.json",
+    "requirements.txt",
+    "backend/requirements.txt",
+    "frontend/package.json",
+    "docker-compose.yml",
+    "docker-compose.yaml",
+    "Makefile",
+)
+
+
+def _run(project: Path, *args: str) -> tuple[int, str]:
+    try:
+        completed = subprocess.run(
+            args,
+            cwd=project,
+            capture_output=True,
+            text=True,
+            encoding="utf-8",
+            errors="replace",
+            timeout=20,
+        )
+    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as exc:
+        return 1, str(exc)
+    return completed.returncode, completed.stdout.strip()
+
+
+def _redact(text: str) -> str:
+    """Remove common credential forms before a capsule is written."""
+    patterns = (
+        r"(?i)(api[_-]?key|token|password|secret)\s*([=:])\s*[^\s'\"`]+",
+        r"\bsk-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{12,}\b",
+        r"\beyJ[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}\b",
+    )
+    for pattern in patterns:
+        text = re.sub(pattern, "<REDACTED>", text)
+    return text
+
+
+def _excerpt(path: Path, limit: int) -> str:
+    try:
+        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
+    except OSError as exc:
+        return f"[Could not read: {exc}]"
+    text = _redact(text).strip()
+    if len(text) <= limit:
+        return text
+    # Keep the beginning, which normally contains scope and instructions, and
+    # label the loss so a receiver does not mistake it for a complete document.
+    return text[:limit].rstrip() + f"\n\n[Excerpt truncated at {limit:,} characters; inspect {path.name} before relying on omitted detail.]"
+
+
+def _count_files(project: Path) -> int:
+    count = 0
+    for path in project.rglob("*"):
+        if any(part in IGNORED_DIRS for part in path.parts):
+            continue
+        if path.is_file():
+            count += 1
+    return count
+
+
+def _git_state(project: Path) -> tuple[list[str], str, str]:
+    code, root = _run(project, "git", "rev-parse", "--show-toplevel")
+    if code:
+        return [], "Not a Git repository (or git unavailable).", ""
+    _, branch = _run(project, "git", "branch", "--show-current")
+    _, head = _run(project, "git", "log", "-1", "--format=%H")
+    _, log = _run(project, "git", "log", "-8", "--date=short", "--format=%h %ad %s")
+    _, porcelain = _run(project, "git", "status", "--porcelain=v1", "-uall")
+    changed = porcelain.splitlines() if porcelain else []
+
+    def cache_shaped(entry: str) -> bool:
+        candidate = entry[3:].replace("\\", "/").lower()
+        return any(
+            marker in candidate
+            for marker in (
+                "/.edge-render-",
+                "/crashpad/",
+                "/cache/",
+                "/code cache/",
+                "/gpucache/",
+                "/dawngraphitecache/",
+                "/dawnwebgpucache/",
+            )
+        )
+
+    cache_paths = [entry for entry in changed if cache_shaped(entry)]
+    actionable = [entry for entry in changed if entry not in cache_paths]
+    status = [
+        f"Git root: `{root}`",
+        f"Branch: `{branch or '(detached HEAD)'}`",
+        f"HEAD: `{head or '(unavailable)'}`",
+        f"Working tree: {'clean' if not changed else f'dirty ({len(changed)} paths)'}",
+    ]
+    if changed:
+        status.append(
+            f"Changed-path triage: {len(actionable)} non-cache-shaped paths; "
+            f"{len(cache_paths)} browser/cache-shaped paths (not assumed disposable)."
+        )
+        shown = actionable[:80]
+        if shown:
+            status.append("Non-cache-shaped changed paths (porcelain; inspect before editing):\n```text\n" + "\n".join(shown) + "\n```")
+        if len(actionable) > len(shown):
+            status.append(f"[Only first {len(shown)} of {len(actionable)} non-cache-shaped paths shown.]")
+        if cache_paths:
+            status.append(
+                "[Browser/cache-shaped paths are summarized to keep the handoff legible; "
+                "they remain part of the dirty tree and must not be deleted blindly.]"
+            )
+    return status, log or "(No commits found.)", branch
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class CapsuleResult:
+    project: Path
+    output: Path
+    chars: int
+    included_documents: tuple[str, ...]
+
+
+def build_capsule(project: Path, output: Path, *, max_chars: int = DEFAULT_MAX_CHARS) -> CapsuleResult:
+    """Write one Markdown capsule.  Does not mutate *project*."""
+    project = project.expanduser().resolve()
+    output = output.expanduser().resolve()
+    if not project.is_dir():
+        raise ValueError(f"project is not a directory: {project}")
+    if max_chars < 4_000:
+        raise ValueError("max_chars must be at least 4000")
+
+    git_state, recent_log, branch = _git_state(project)
+    top_level = sorted(p.name + ("/" if p.is_dir() else "") for p in project.iterdir() if p.name not in IGNORED_DIRS)
+    lines = [
+        "# Project Capsule",
+        "",
+        "> Generated context, not a source of truth. Verify cited files and current Git state before changing the project.",
+        "",
+        "## Receiver protocol",
+        "",
+        "1. Treat the project instructions and constraints below as binding.",
+        "2. Preserve existing dirty work: inspect it before editing or cleaning anything.",
+        "3. Reconcile any conflicting roadmap/current-state material by checking Git history and the cited source files.",
+        "4. Before implementation, read the listed files most relevant to the chosen change and run the narrowest documented check.",
+        "",
+        "## Observed repository",
+        "",
+        f"- Project: `{project}`",
+        f"- Generated (UTC): `{datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()}`",
+        f"- Approximate tracked/working source files (excluding common dependency/cache directories): {_count_files(project):,}",
+        f"- Top level: {', '.join(f'`{name}`' for name in top_level[:60])}" + (" …" if len(top_level) > 60 else ""),
+        "",
+        "## Git working situation",
+        "",
+        *git_state,
+        "",
+        "### Recent commits",
+        "",
+        "```text",
+        recent_log,
+        "```",
+        "",
+        "## Runtime / build manifests",
+        "",
+    ]
+    for rel in MANIFEST_CANDIDATES:
+        path = project / rel
+        if path.is_file():
+            lines.extend((f"### `{rel}`", "", "```text", _excerpt(path, 3_000), "```", ""))
+
+    included: list[str] = []
+    # Reserve room for headings and the source map. Individual excerpts are
+    # additionally bounded; the global check below never silently overflows.
+    for category, rel, limit in DOCUMENT_CANDIDATES:
+        path = project / rel
+        if not path.is_file():
+            continue
+        remaining = max_chars - len("\n".join(lines)) - 1_600
+        if remaining < 500:
+            break
+        excerpt = _excerpt(path, min(limit, max(500, remaining)))
+        lines.extend((f"## {category}: `{rel}`", "", excerpt, ""))
+        included.append(rel)
+
+    lines.extend((
+        "## What to inspect before changing code",
+        "",
+        "- The mandatory instruction files above, plus any documents named by them for the surface you will change.",
+        "- The current diff for every dirty path that overlaps your task; do not assume it is disposable.",
+        "- The owning backend/frontend module and its nearest existing smoke or test script.",
+        "- Deployment/production gates before touching deploy, Caddy, provider configuration, or a manual-test gate.",
+        "",
+        "## Capsule boundaries",
+        "",
+        "- This is a compact index and excerpts, not an archive, a transcript, or a substitute for source inspection.",
+        "- It intentionally omits untracked file contents, Git diff bodies, binaries, dependency trees, and environment files.",
+        "- Common credential-shaped values are redacted heuristically; never treat a capsule as a safe place for secrets.",
+        "- `current` and `next` claims remain attributed to the included documents and Git observation; documents can be stale or conflict.",
+        "",
+        "## Included source map",
+        "",
+        *[f"- `{item}`" for item in included],
+    ))
+    text = "\n".join(lines).rstrip() + "\n"
+    if len(text) > max_chars:
+        # This should be rare because documents are budgeted. Preserve a valid,
+        # explicitly incomplete result instead of failing after expensive scan.
+        text = text[: max_chars - 120].rstrip() + "\n\n[Capsule reached global size limit; inspect project files directly.]\n"
+    output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    output.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
+    return CapsuleResult(project=project, output=output, chars=len(text), included_documents=tuple(included))

+    )

+                "chars": result.chars,
+                "included_documents": list(result.included_documents),
+            },
+            indent=2,
+        )
+    )

+"""Tests for compact, source-grounded project handoff capsules."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import os
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "src"))
+
+from proteus.capsule import build_capsule  # noqa: E402
+
+
+def run(project: Path, *args: str) -> None:
+    subprocess.run(args, cwd=project, check=True, capture_output=True, text=True)
+
+
+def test_capsule_contains_handoff_essentials_and_redacts() -> None:
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+        root = Path(td)
+        project = root / "project"
+        project.mkdir()
+        (project / "AGENTS.md").write_text("# Rules\nRead docs/law.md first.\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        (project / "README.md").write_text("# Example\nA practical application.\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        (project / "PROJECT_INTENT_AND_CURRENT_STATE.md").write_text(
+            "# Current\nThe active focus is migration safety.\nAPI_KEY=should_not_escape\n",
+            encoding="utf-8",
+        )
+        (project / "ROADMAP_NEXT_STEPS.md").write_text(
+            "# Next\nRun the targeted migration smoke before a broad suite.\n",
+            encoding="utf-8",
+        )
+        (project / "package.json").write_text('{"name":"example"}\n', encoding="utf-8")
+        (project / "changed.py").write_text("before\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        run(project, "git", "init")
+        run(project, "git", "config", "user.email", "test@example.invalid")
+        run(project, "git", "config", "user.name", "Capsule Test")
+        run(project, "git", "add", ".")
+        run(project, "git", "commit", "-m", "initial")
+        (project / "changed.py").write_text("after\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        (project / "untracked.txt").write_text("local work\n", encoding="utf-8")
+
+        out = root / "capsule.md"
+        result = build_capsule(project, out, max_chars=12_000)
+        text = out.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+        assert result.output == out.resolve()
+        assert result.chars == len(text)
+        assert "Read docs/law.md first" in text
+        assert "active focus is migration safety" in text
+        assert "targeted migration smoke" in text
+        assert "Working tree: dirty (2 paths)" in text
+        assert "Changed-path triage: 2 non-cache-shaped paths" in text
+        assert "changed.py" in text and "untracked.txt" in text
+        assert "should_not_escape" not in text
+        assert "<REDACTED>" in text
+        assert "What to inspect before changing code" in text
+
+
+def test_capsule_respects_global_bound_and_does_not_mutate_project() -> None:
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+        root = Path(td)
+        project = root / "project"
+        project.mkdir()
+        original = "# Readme\n" + ("useful context\n" * 2_000)
+        (project / "README.md").write_text(original, encoding="utf-8")
+        out = root / "capsule.md"
+        result = build_capsule(project, out, max_chars=4_500)
+        assert result.chars <= 4_500
+        assert (project / "README.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8") == original
+        assert "Generated context, not a source of truth" in out.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+
+
+def test_capsule_summarizes_cache_shaped_dirty_paths() -> None:
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+        root = Path(td)
+        project = root / "project"
+        cache = project / "SAS" / ".edge-render-x" / "Default" / "Cache"
+        cache.mkdir(parents=True)
+        (project / "README.md").write_text("# Example\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        (project / "useful.txt").write_text("keep me\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        cache_file = cache / "blob"
+        cache_file.write_text("generated\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        run(project, "git", "init")
+        run(project, "git", "config", "user.email", "test@example.invalid")
+        run(project, "git", "config", "user.name", "Capsule Test")

+        out = root / "capsule.md"

+        text = out.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+        assert "1 non-cache-shaped paths; 1 browser/cache-shaped paths" in text
+        assert "useful.txt" in text
+        assert "Browser/cache-shaped paths are summarized" in text
+
+
+def main() -> None:
+    test_capsule_contains_handoff_essentials_and_redacts()
+    test_capsule_respects_global_bound_and_does_not_mutate_project()
+    test_capsule_summarizes_cache_shaped_dirty_paths()

+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()

+"""Create a compact, source-grounded handoff capsule for a coding project.
+
+The capsule deliberately collects only durable entry material and observable git
+state.  It is not an archive and it does not attempt to infer a task from an
+agent transcript.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import re
+import subprocess
+from dataclasses import dataclass
+from datetime import datetime, timezone
+from pathlib import Path
+
+
+DEFAULT_MAX_CHARS = 48_000
+IGNORED_DIRS = {".git", ".next", "node_modules", ".venv", "__pycache__"}
+
+# Ordered from mandatory project instructions through likely current-work
+# carriers.  A missing file is normal; projects vary widely.
+DOCUMENT_CANDIDATES = (
+    ("Project instructions", "AGENTS.md", 3_000),
+    ("Project instructions", "CLAUDE.md", 3_000),
+    ("Operations constraints", "docs/ops/PRODUCTION_TOPOLOGY_AND_GATES.md", 4_500),
+    ("Product law", "docs/product/MODERATOR_COCKPIT_INTERFACE_LAW_v0_1.md", 5_000),
+    ("Project overview", "README.md", 3_000),
+    ("Current-state carrier", "CURRENT.md", 5_000),
+    ("Current-state carrier", "FRONTIER.md", 5_000),
+    ("Current-state carrier", "PROJECT_INTENT_AND_CURRENT_STATE.md", 7_000),
+    ("Current plan referenced by roadmap", "RECOVERY_REENTRY_MASTER_PLAN_v2_3_to_v3_4.md", 6_000),
+    ("Roadmap / next work", "ROADMAP_NEXT_STEPS.md", 5_000),
+    ("Handoff", "HANDOFF.md", 5_000),
+    ("Contribution guide", "CONTRIBUTING.md", 4_000),
+)
+MANIFEST_CANDIDATES = (
+    "pyproject.toml",
+    "package.json",
+    "requirements.txt",
+    "backend/requirements.txt",
+    "frontend/package.json",
+    "docker-compose.yml",
+    "docker-compose.yaml",
+    "Makefile",
+)
+
+
+def _run(project: Path, *args: str) -> tuple[int, str]:
+    try:
+        completed = subprocess.run(
+            args,
+            cwd=project,
+            capture_output=True,
+            text=True,
+            encoding="utf-8",
+            errors="replace",
+            timeout=20,
+        )
+    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as exc:
+        return 1, str(exc)
+    return completed.returncode, completed.stdout.strip()
+
+
+def _redact(text: str) -> str:
+    """Remove common credential forms before a capsule is written."""
+    patterns = (
+        r"(?i)(api[_-]?key|token|password|secret)\s*([=:])\s*[^\s'\"`]+",
+        r"\bsk-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{12,}\b",
+        r"\beyJ[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}\b",
+    )
+    for pattern in patterns:
+        text = re.sub(pattern, "<REDACTED>", text)
+    return text
+
+
+def _excerpt(path: Path, limit: int) -> str:
+    try:
+        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
+    except OSError as exc:
+        return f"[Could not read: {exc}]"
+    text = _redact(text).strip()
+    if len(text) <= limit:
+        return text
+    # Keep the beginning, which normally contains scope and instructions, and
+    # label the loss so a receiver does not mistake it for a complete document.
+    return text[:limit].rstrip() + f"\n\n[Excerpt truncated at {limit:,} characters; inspect {path.name} before relying on omitted detail.]"
+
+
+def _count_files(project: Path) -> int:
+    count = 0
+    for path in project.rglob("*"):
+        if any(part in IGNORED_DIRS for part in path.parts):
+            continue
+        if path.is_file():
+            count += 1
+    return count
+
+
+def _git_state(project: Path) -> tuple[list[str], str, str]:
+    code, root = _run(project, "git", "rev-parse", "--show-toplevel")
+    if code:
+        return [], "Not a Git repository (or git unavailable).", ""
+    _, branch = _run(project, "git", "branch", "--show-current")
+    _, head = _run(project, "git", "log", "-1", "--format=%H")
+    _, log = _run(project, "git", "log", "-8", "--date=short", "--format=%h %ad %s")
+    _, porcelain = _run(project, "git", "status", "--porcelain=v1", "-uall")
+    changed = porcelain.splitlines() if porcelain else []
+
+    def cache_shaped(entry: str) -> bool:
+        candidate = entry[3:].replace("\\", "/").lower()
+        return any(
+            marker in candidate
+            for marker in (
+                "/.edge-render-",
+                "/crashpad/",
+                "/cache/",
+                "/code cache/",
+                "/gpucache/",
+                "/dawngraphitecache/",
+                "/dawnwebgpucache/",
+            )
+        )
+
+    cache_paths = [entry for entry in changed if cache_shaped(entry)]
+    actionable = [entry for entry in changed if entry not in cache_paths]
+    status = [
+        f"Git root: `{root}`",
+        f"Branch: `{branch or '(detached HEAD)'}`",
+        f"HEAD: `{head or '(unavailable)'}`",
+        f"Working tree: {'clean' if not changed else f'dirty ({len(changed)} paths)'}",
+    ]
+    if changed:
+        status.append(
+            f"Changed-path triage: {len(actionable)} non-cache-shaped paths; "
+            f"{len(cache_paths)} browser/cache-shaped paths (not assumed disposable)."
+        )
+        shown = actionable[:80]
+        if shown:
+            status.append("Non-cache-shaped changed paths (porcelain; inspect before editing):\n```text\n" + "\n".join(shown) + "\n```")
+        if len(actionable) > len(shown):
+            status.append(f"[Only first {len(shown)} of {len(actionable)} non-cache-shaped paths shown.]")
+        if cache_paths:
+            status.append(
+                "[Browser/cache-shaped paths are summarized to keep the handoff legible; "
+                "they remain part of the dirty tree and must not be deleted blindly.]"
+            )
+    return status, log or "(No commits found.)", branch
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class CapsuleResult:
+    project: Path
+    output: Path
+    chars: int
+    included_documents: tuple[str, ...]
+
+
+def build_capsule(project: Path, output: Path, *, max_chars: int = DEFAULT_MAX_CHARS) -> CapsuleResult:
+    """Write one Markdown capsule.  Does not mutate *project*."""
+    project = project.expanduser().resolve()
+    output = output.expanduser().resolve()
+    if not project.is_dir():
+        raise ValueError(f"project is not a directory: {project}")
+    if max_chars < 4_000:
+        raise ValueError("max_chars must be at least 4000")
+
+    git_state, recent_log, branch = _git_state(project)
+    top_level = sorted(p.name + ("/" if p.is_dir() else "") for p in project.iterdir() if p.name not in IGNORED_DIRS)
+    lines = [
+        "# Project Capsule",
+        "",
+        "> Generated context, not a source of truth. Verify cited files and current Git state before changing the project.",
+        "",
+        "## Receiver protocol",
+        "",
+        "1. Treat the project instructions and constraints below as binding.",
+        "2. Preserve existing dirty work: inspect it before editing or cleaning anything.",
+        "3. Reconcile any conflicting roadmap/current-state material by checking Git history and the cited source files.",
+        "4. Before implementation, read the listed files most relevant to the chosen change and run the narrowest documented check.",
+        "",
+        "## Observed repository",
+        "",
+        f"- Project: `{project}`",
+        f"- Generated (UTC): `{datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()}`",
+        f"- Approximate tracked/working source files (excluding common dependency/cache directories): {_count_files(project):,}",
+        f"- Top level: {', '.join(f'`{name}`' for name in top_level[:60])}" + (" …" if len(top_level) > 60 else ""),
+        "",
+        "## Git working situation",
+        "",
+        *git_state,
+        "",
+        "### Recent commits",
+        "",
+        "```text",
+        recent_log,
+        "```",
+        "",
+        "## Runtime / build manifests",
+        "",
+    ]
+    for rel in MANIFEST_CANDIDATES:
+        path = project / rel
+        if path.is_file():
+            lines.extend((f"### `{rel}`", "", "```text", _excerpt(path, 3_000), "```", ""))
+
+    included: list[str] = []
+    # Reserve room for headings and the source map. Individual excerpts are
+    # additionally bounded; the global check below never silently overflows.
+    for category, rel, limit in DOCUMENT_CANDIDATES:
+        path = project / rel
+        if not path.is_file():
+            continue
+        remaining = max_chars - len("\n".join(lines)) - 1_600
+        if remaining < 500:
+            break
+        excerpt = _excerpt(path, min(limit, max(500, remaining)))
+        lines.extend((f"## {category}: `{rel}`", "", excerpt, ""))
+        included.append(rel)
+
+    lines.extend((
+        "## What to inspect before changing code",
+        "",
+        "- The mandatory instruction files above, plus any documents named by them for the surface you will change.",
+        "- The current diff for every dirty path that overlaps your task; do not assume it is disposable.",
+        "- The owning backend/frontend module and its nearest existing smoke or test script.",
+        "- Deployment/production gates before touching deploy, Caddy, provider configuration, or a manual-test gate.",
+        "",
+        "## Capsule boundaries",
+        "",
+        "- This is a compact index and excerpts, not an archive, a transcript, or a substitute for source inspection.",
+        "- It intentionally omits untracked file contents, Git diff bodies, binaries, dependency trees, and environment files.",
+        "- Common credential-shaped values are redacted heuristically; never treat a capsule as a safe place for secrets.",
+        "- `current` and `next` claims remain attributed to the included documents and Git observation; documents can be stale or conflict.",
+        "",
+        "## Included source map",
+        "",
+        *[f"- `{item}`" for item in included],
+    ))
+    text = "\n".join(lines).rstrip() + "\n"
+    if len(text) > max_chars:
+        # This should be rare because documents are budgeted. Preserve a valid,
+        # explicitly incomplete result instead of failing after expensive scan.
+        text = text[: max_chars - 120].rstrip() + "\n\n[Capsule reached global size limit; inspect project files directly.]\n"
+    output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    output.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
+    return CapsuleResult(project=project, output=output, chars=len(text), included_documents=tuple(included))

+"""Tests for compact, source-grounded project handoff capsules."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import os
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "src"))
+
+from proteus.capsule import build_capsule  # noqa: E402
+
+
+def run(project: Path, *args: str) -> None:
+    subprocess.run(args, cwd=project, check=True, capture_output=True, text=True)
+
+
+def test_capsule_contains_handoff_essentials_and_redacts() -> None:
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+        root = Path(td)
+        project = root / "project"
+        project.mkdir()
+        (project / "AGENTS.md").write_text("# Rules\nRead docs/law.md first.\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        (project / "README.md").write_text("# Example\nA practical application.\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        (project / "PROJECT_INTENT_AND_CURRENT_STATE.md").write_text(
+            "# Current\nThe active focus is migration safety.\nAPI_KEY=should_not_escape\n",
+            encoding="utf-8",
+        )
+        (project / "ROADMAP_NEXT_STEPS.md").write_text(
+            "# Next\nRun the targeted migration smoke before a broad suite.\n",
+            encoding="utf-8",
+        )
+        (project / "package.json").write_text('{"name":"example"}\n', encoding="utf-8")
+        (project / "changed.py").write_text("before\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        run(project, "git", "init")
+        run(project, "git", "config", "user.email", "test@example.invalid")
+        run(project, "git", "config", "user.name", "Capsule Test")
+        run(project, "git", "add", ".")
+        run(project, "git", "commit", "-m", "initial")
+        (project / "changed.py").write_text("after\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        (project / "untracked.txt").write_text("local work\n", encoding="utf-8")
+
+        out = root / "capsule.md"
+        result = build_capsule(project, out, max_chars=12_000)
+        text = out.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+        assert result.output == out.resolve()
+        assert result.chars == len(text)
+        assert "Read docs/law.md first" in text
+        assert "active focus is migration safety" in text
+        assert "targeted migration smoke" in text
+        assert "Working tree: dirty (2 paths)" in text
+        assert "Changed-path triage: 2 non-cache-shaped paths" in text
+        assert "changed.py" in text and "untracked.txt" in text
+        assert "should_not_escape" not in text
+        assert "<REDACTED>" in text
+        assert "What to inspect before changing code" in text
+
+
+def test_capsule_respects_global_bound_and_does_not_mutate_project() -> None:
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+        root = Path(td)
+        project = root / "project"
+        project.mkdir()
+        original = "# Readme\n" + ("useful context\n" * 2_000)
+        (project / "README.md").write_text(original, encoding="utf-8")
+        out = root / "capsule.md"
+        result = build_capsule(project, out, max_chars=4_500)
+        assert result.chars <= 4_500
+        assert (project / "README.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8") == original
+        assert "Generated context, not a source of truth" in out.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+
+
+def test_capsule_summarizes_cache_shaped_dirty_paths() -> None:
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+        root = Path(td)
+        project = root / "project"
+        cache = project / "SAS" / ".edge-render-x" / "Default" / "Cache"
+        cache.mkdir(parents=True)
+        (project / "README.md").write_text("# Example\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        (project / "useful.txt").write_text("keep me\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        cache_file = cache / "blob"
+        cache_file.write_text("generated\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        run(project, "git", "init")
+        run(project, "git", "config", "user.email", "test@example.invalid")
+        run(project, "git", "config", "user.name", "Capsule Test")
+        run(project, "git", "add", ".")
+        run(project, "git", "commit", "-m", "initial")
+        (project / "useful.txt").write_text("changed\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        cache_file.write_text("changed cache\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        out = root / "capsule.md"
+        build_capsule(project, out)
+        text = out.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+        assert "1 non-cache-shaped paths; 1 browser/cache-shaped paths" in text
+        assert "useful.txt" in text
+        assert "Browser/cache-shaped paths are summarized" in text
+
+
+def main() -> None:
+    test_capsule_contains_handoff_essentials_and_redacts()
+    test_capsule_respects_global_bound_and_does_not_mutate_project()
+    test_capsule_summarizes_cache_shaped_dirty_paths()
+    print("test_capsule: PASS")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()

+# F05 project capsule episode
+
+Date: 2026-09-11
+
+## Bounded capability
+
+Added `python -m proteus capsule --project <existing-project> --out <capsule.md>`.
+It creates one bounded Markdown handoff artifact without writing to the selected
+project. The artifact records observable Git state, runtime manifests, required
+instructions, and prioritized excerpts of standard current-state and roadmap
+carriers. It is intentionally not an archive or transcript.
+
+## Exercise target
+
+Selected `C:\projects\multiagent-moderation-env` (selection rationale:
+`F05_PROJECT_SELECTION.md`). The generated artifact is
+`F05_MULTIAGENT_MODERATION_PROJECT_CAPSULE.md`.
+
+Final generation observation:
+
+- 38,527 Unicode characters (45,949 UTF-8 bytes), substantially smaller than
+  the target tree.
+- The target had 4,207 non-dependency/cache files by the capsule's inventory.
+- Git observation: `main` at `e52389098cb21d9d65a6998702253a7535e25872`,
+  dirty with 3,075 paths.
+- The capsule separated 324 non-cache-shaped paths from 2,751
+  browser/cache-shaped paths; it did not classify either group as safe to
+  delete.
+
+## Independent receiver
+
+An independent `codex exec --ephemeral` receiver was launched with no resume,
+fork, or prior transcript. Its only supplied project-context artifact was the
+first generated capsule; it was explicitly asked not to inspect source or
+modify the target. Input: `F05_CAPSULE_RECEIVER_PROMPT.txt`; raw preserved
+answer: `F05_CAPSULE_RECEIVER_RAW_RESULT.txt`; launch receipt:
+`F05_CAPSULE_RECEIVER_META.json`.
+
+The receiver correctly understood the product as a FastAPI/SQLAlchemy/Postgres
++ Next.js moderator workspace and identified dirty work, stale/conflicting
+roadmap signals, production LLM gating, and the need to inspect governing docs,
+diffs, owning modules, smoke scripts, and the master recovery plan before a
+change.
+
+## Causal repair after receiver review
+
+The first capsule's first-80 Git-path listing was dominated by browser profile
+and cache artifacts, obscuring the actionable dirty set. Also, the roadmap
+named `RECOVERY_REENTRY_MASTER_PLAN_v2_3_to_v3_4.md` as its source of truth but
+the capsule did not include it. The implementation now:
+
+1. summarizes browser/cache-shaped dirty paths separately while keeping an
+   explicit warning that they are not presumed disposable;
+2. prioritizes mandatory AGENTS/operations/product-law documents; and
+3. includes the named master recovery plan before the roadmap excerpt.
+
+The repaired final capsule was regenerated at the path above. The raw receiver
+answer remains untouched as the first-pass evaluation record.
+
+## Validation
+
+- `python tests/test_capsule.py` — PASS
+- `python -m pytest -q` — 50 passed
+- `python -m proteus capsule --help` — PASS
+- `git diff --check` — PASS (only line-ending warnings)
+
+## Known boundaries
+
+- Standard document names are intentionally prioritized; unusual project-local
+  state carriers require adding a candidate or inspecting source directly.
+- Redaction is heuristic and must never be treated as a permission to include
+  secrets in a capsule.
+- The capsule exposes Git paths and document excerpts, not diff bodies or
+  untracked-file contents. A receiver still must inspect relevant source before
+  editing.
+- The target's current-state documents contain historical and conflicting
+  sequencing. The capsule identifies this instead of selecting a false single
+  truth.

+"""Create a compact, source-grounded handoff capsule for a coding project.
+
+The capsule deliberately collects only durable entry material and observable git
+state.  It is not an archive and it does not attempt to infer a task from an
+agent transcript.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import re
+import subprocess
+from dataclasses import dataclass
+from datetime import datetime, timezone
+from pathlib import Path
+
+
+DEFAULT_MAX_CHARS = 48_000
+IGNORED_DIRS = {".git", ".next", "node_modules", ".venv", "__pycache__"}
+
+# Ordered from mandatory project instructions through likely current-work
+# carriers.  A missing file is normal; projects vary widely.
+DOCUMENT_CANDIDATES = (
+    ("Project instructions", "AGENTS.md", 3_000),
+    ("Project instructions", "CLAUDE.md", 3_000),
+    ("Operations constraints", "docs/ops/PRODUCTION_TOPOLOGY_AND_GATES.md", 4_500),
+    ("Product law", "docs/product/MODERATOR_COCKPIT_INTERFACE_LAW_v0_1.md", 5_000),
+    ("Project overview", "README.md", 3_000),
+    ("Current-state carrier", "CURRENT.md", 5_000),
+    ("Current-state carrier", "FRONTIER.md", 5_000),
+    ("Current-state carrier", "PROJECT_INTENT_AND_CURRENT_STATE.md", 7_000),
+    ("Current plan referenced by roadmap", "RECOVERY_REENTRY_MASTER_PLAN_v2_3_to_v3_4.md", 6_000),
+    ("Roadmap / next work", "ROADMAP_NEXT_STEPS.md", 5_000),
+    ("Handoff", "HANDOFF.md", 5_000),
+    ("Contribution guide", "CONTRIBUTING.md", 4_000),
+)
+MANIFEST_CANDIDATES = (
+    "pyproject.toml",
+    "package.json",
+    "requirements.txt",
+    "backend/requirements.txt",
+    "frontend/package.json",
+    "docker-compose.yml",
+    "docker-compose.yaml",
+    "Makefile",
+)
+
+
+def _run(project: Path, *args: str) -> tuple[int, str]:
+    try:
+        completed = subprocess.run(
+            args,
+            cwd=project,
+            capture_output=True,
+            text=True,
+            encoding="utf-8",
+            errors="replace",
+            timeout=20,
+        )
+    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as exc:
+        return 1, str(exc)
+    return completed.returncode, completed.stdout.strip()
+
+
+def _redact(text: str) -> str:
+    """Remove common credential forms before a capsule is written."""
+    patterns = (
+        r"(?i)(api[_-]?key|token|password|secret)\s*([=:])\s*[^\s'\"`]+",
+        r"\bsk-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{12,}\b",
+        r"\beyJ[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}\b",
+    )
+    for pattern in patterns:
+        text = re.sub(pattern, "<REDACTED>", text)
+    return text
+
+
+def _excerpt(path: Path, limit: int) -> str:
+    try:
+        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
+    except OSError as exc:
+        return f"[Could not read: {exc}]"
+    text = _redact(text).strip()
+    if len(text) <= limit:
+        return text
+    # Keep the beginning, which normally contains scope and instructions, and
+    # label the loss so a receiver does not mistake it for a complete document.
+    return text[:limit].rstrip() + f"\n\n[Excerpt truncated at {limit:,} characters; inspect {path.name} before relying on omitted detail.]"
+
+
+def _count_files(project: Path) -> int:
+    count = 0
+    for path in project.rglob("*"):
+        if any(part in IGNORED_DIRS for part in path.parts):
+            continue
+        if path.is_file():
+            count += 1
+    return count
+
+
+def _git_state(project: Path) -> tuple[list[str], str, str]:
+    code, root = _run(project, "git", "rev-parse", "--show-toplevel")
+    if code:
+        return [], "Not a Git repository (or git unavailable).", ""
+    _, branch = _run(project, "git", "branch", "--show-current")
+    _, head = _run(project, "git", "log", "-1", "--format=%H")
+    _, log = _run(project, "git", "log", "-8", "--date=short", "--format=%h %ad %s")
+    _, porcelain = _run(project, "git", "status", "--porcelain=v1", "-uall")
+    changed = porcelain.splitlines() if porcelain else []
+
+    def cache_shaped(entry: str) -> bool:
+        candidate = entry[3:].replace("\\", "/").lower()
+        return any(
+            marker in candidate
+            for marker in (
+                "/.edge-render-",
+                "/crashpad/",
+                "/cache/",
+                "/code cache/",
+                "/gpucache/",
+                "/dawngraphitecache/",
+                "/dawnwebgpucache/",
+            )
+        )
+
+    cache_paths = [entry for entry in changed if cache_shaped(entry)]
+    actionable = [entry for entry in changed if entry not in cache_paths]
+    status = [
+        f"Git root: `{root}`",
+        f"Branch: `{branch or '(detached HEAD)'}`",
+        f"HEAD: `{head or '(unavailable)'}`",
+        f"Working tree: {'clean' if not changed else f'dirty ({len(changed)} paths)'}",
+    ]
+    if changed:
+        status.append(
+            f"Changed-path triage: {len(actionable)} non-cache-shaped paths; "
+            f"{len(cache_paths)} browser/cache-shaped paths (not assumed disposable)."
+        )
+        shown = actionable[:80]
+        if shown:
+            status.append("Non-cache-shaped changed paths (porcelain; inspect before editing):\n```text\n" + "\n".join(shown) + "\n```")
+        if len(actionable) > len(shown):
+            status.append(f"[Only first {len(shown)} of {len(actionable)} non-cache-shaped paths shown.]")
+        if cache_paths:
+            status.append(
+                "[Browser/cache-shaped paths are summarized to keep the handoff legible; "
+                "they remain part of the dirty tree and must not be deleted blindly.]"
+            )
+    return status, log or "(No commits found.)", branch
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class CapsuleResult:
+    project: Path
+    output: Path
+    chars: int
+    included_documents: tuple[str, ...]
+
+
+def build_capsule(project: Path, output: Path, *, max_chars: int = DEFAULT_MAX_CHARS) -> CapsuleResult:
+    """Write one Markdown capsule.  Does not mutate *project*."""
+    project = project.expanduser().resolve()
+    output = output.expanduser().resolve()
+    if not project.is_dir():
+        raise ValueError(f"project is not a directory: {project}")
+    if max_chars < 4_000:
+        raise ValueError("max_chars must be at least 4000")
+
+    git_state, recent_log, branch = _git_state(project)
+    top_level = sorted(p.name + ("/" if p.is_dir() else "") for p in project.iterdir() if p.name not in IGNORED_DIRS)
+    lines = [
+        "# Project Capsule",
+        "",
+        "> Generated context, not a source of truth. Verify cited files and current Git state before changing the project.",
+        "",
+        "## Receiver protocol",
+        "",
+        "1. Treat the project instructions and constraints below as binding.",
+        "2. Preserve existing dirty work: inspect it before editing or cleaning anything.",
+        "3. Reconcile any conflicting roadmap/current-state material by checking Git history and the cited source files.",
+        "4. Before implementation, read the listed files most relevant to the chosen change and run the narrowest documented check.",
+        "",
+        "## Observed repository",
+        "",
+        f"- Project: `{project}`",
+        f"- Generated (UTC): `{datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()}`",
+        f"- Approximate tracked/working source files (excluding common dependency/cache directories): {_count_files(project):,}",
+        f"- Top level: {', '.join(f'`{name}`' for name in top_level[:60])}" + (" …" if len(top_level) > 60 else ""),
+        "",
+        "## Git working situation",
+        "",
+        *git_state,
+        "",
+        "### Recent commits",
+        "",
+        "```text",
+        recent_log,
+        "```",
+        "",
+        "## Runtime / build manifests",
+        "",
+    ]
+    for rel in MANIFEST_CANDIDATES:
+        path = project / rel
+        if path.is_file():
+            lines.extend((f"### `{rel}`", "", "```text", _excerpt(path, 3_000), "```", ""))
+
+    included: list[str] = []
+    # Reserve room for headings and the source map. Individual excerpts are
+    # additionally bounded; the global check below never silently overflows.
+    for category, rel, limit in DOCUMENT_CANDIDATES:
+        path = project / rel
+        if not path.is_file():
+            continue
+        remaining = max_chars - len("\n".join(lines)) - 1_600
+        if remaining < 500:
+            break
+        excerpt = _excerpt(path, min(limit, max(500, remaining)))
+        lines.extend((f"## {category}: `{rel}`", "", excerpt, ""))
+        included.append(rel)
+
+    lines.extend((
+        "## What to inspect before changing code",
+        "",
+        "- The mandatory instruction files above, plus any documents named by them for the surface you will change.",
+        "- The current diff for every dirty path that overlaps your task; do not assume it is disposable.",
+        "- The owning backend/frontend module and its nearest existing smoke or test script.",
+        "- Deployment/production gates before touching deploy, Caddy, provider configuration, or a manual-test gate.",
+        "",
+        "## Capsule boundaries",
+        "",
+        "- This is a compact index and excerpts, not an archive, a transcript, or a substitute for source inspection.",
+        "- It intentionally omits untracked file contents, Git diff bodies, binaries, dependency trees, and environment files.",
+        "- Common credential-shaped values are redacted heuristically; never treat a capsule as a safe place for secrets.",
+        "- `current` and `next` claims remain attributed to the included documents and Git observation; documents can be stale or conflict.",
+        "",
+        "## Included source map",
+        "",
+        *[f"- `{item}`" for item in included],
+    ))
+    text = "\n".join(lines).rstrip() + "\n"
+    if len(text) > max_chars:
+        # This should be rare because documents are budgeted. Preserve a valid,
+        # explicitly incomplete result instead of failing after expensive scan.
+        text = text[: max_chars - 120].rstrip() + "\n\n[Capsule reached global size limit; inspect project files directly.]\n"
+    output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    output.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
+    return CapsuleResult(project=project, output=output, chars=len(text), included_documents=tuple(included))

+            },
+            indent=2,
+        )
+    )
+

+"""Tests for compact, source-grounded project handoff capsules."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import os
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "src"))
+
+from proteus.capsule import build_capsule  # noqa: E402
+
+
+def run(project: Path, *args: str) -> None:
+    subprocess.run(args, cwd=project, check=True, capture_output=True, text=True)
+
+
+def test_capsule_contains_handoff_essentials_and_redacts() -> None:
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+        root = Path(td)
+        project = root / "project"
+        project.mkdir()
+        (project / "AGENTS.md").write_text("# Rules\nRead docs/law.md first.\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        (project / "README.md").write_text("# Example\nA practical application.\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        (project / "PROJECT_INTENT_AND_CURRENT_STATE.md").write_text(
+            "# Current\nThe active focus is migration safety.\nAPI_KEY=should_not_escape\n",
+            encoding="utf-8",
+        )
+        (project / "ROADMAP_NEXT_STEPS.md").write_text(
+            "# Next\nRun the targeted migration smoke before a broad suite.\n",
+            encoding="utf-8",
+        )
+        (project / "package.json").write_text('{"name":"example"}\n', encoding="utf-8")
+        (project / "changed.py").write_text("before\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        run(project, "git", "init")
+        run(project, "git", "config", "user.email", "test@example.invalid")
+        run(project, "git", "config", "user.name", "Capsule Test")
+        run(project, "git", "add", ".")
+        run(project, "git", "commit", "-m", "initial")
+        (project / "changed.py").write_text("after\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        (project / "untracked.txt").write_text("local work\n", encoding="utf-8")
+
+        out = root / "capsule.md"
+        result = build_capsule(project, out, max_chars=12_000)
+        text = out.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+        assert result.output == out.resolve()
+        assert result.chars == len(text)
+        assert "Read docs/law.md first" in text
+        assert "active focus is migration safety" in text
+        assert "targeted migration smoke" in text
+        assert "Working tree: dirty (2 paths)" in text
+        assert "Changed-path triage: 2 non-cache-shaped paths" in text
+        assert "changed.py" in text and "untracked.txt" in text
+        assert "should_not_escape" not in text
+        assert "<REDACTED>" in text
+        assert "What to inspect before changing code" in text
+
+
+def test_capsule_respects_global_bound_and_does_not_mutate_project() -> None:
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+        root = Path(td)
+        project = root / "project"
+        project.mkdir()
+        original = "# Readme\n" + ("useful context\n" * 2_000)
+        (project / "README.md").write_text(original, encoding="utf-8")
+        out = root / "capsule.md"
+        result = build_capsule(project, out, max_chars=4_500)
+        assert result.chars <= 4_500
+        assert (project / "README.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8") == original
+        assert "Generated context, not a source of truth" in out.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+
+
+def test_capsule_summarizes_cache_shaped_dirty_paths() -> None:
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+        root = Path(td)
+        project = root / "project"
+        cache = project / "SAS" / ".edge-render-x" / "Default" / "Cache"
+        cache.mkdir(parents=True)
+        (project / "README.md").write_text("# Example\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        (project / "useful.txt").write_text("keep me\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        cache_file = cache / "blob"
+        cache_file.write_text("generated\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        run(project, "git", "init")
+        run(project, "git", "config", "user.email", "test@example.invalid")
+        run(project, "git", "config", "user.name", "Capsule Test")
+        run(project, "git", "add", ".")
+        run(project, "git", "commit", "-m", "initial")
+        (project / "useful.txt").write_text("changed\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        cache_file.write_text("changed cache\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        out = root / "capsule.md"
+        build_capsule(project, out)
+        text = out.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+        assert "1 non-cache-shaped paths; 1 browser/cache-shaped paths" in text
+        assert "useful.txt" in text
+        assert "Browser/cache-shaped paths are summarized" in text
+
+
+def main() -> None:
+    test_capsule_contains_handoff_essentials_and_redacts()
+    test_capsule_respects_global_bound_and_does_not_mutate_project()
+    test_capsule_summarizes_cache_shaped_dirty_paths()
+    print("test_capsule: PASS")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()

 (`PYTHONPATH=src` if needed.) Writes Proteus-owned surfaces and `.proteus/ENTRY.md`; does not overwrite an existing `AGENTS.md` or non-`proteus-*` Cursor rules. Not Gate 1.
+
+Project capsule (compact, practical context for a fresh coding agent):
+
+```
+python -m proteus capsule --project C:\path\to\project --out C:\handoff\PROJECT_CAPSULE.md
+```
+
+The command is read-only with respect to the selected project. It records observed Git state, manifests, mandatory instructions, and bounded excerpts from standard overview/current-state/roadmap documents. Give the Markdown file to the fresh agent as project context, then have it inspect the cited sources before editing. It deliberately omits diff bodies, untracked file contents, binaries, dependency trees, environment files, and agent transcripts; credential-shaped strings in included excerpts are heuristically redacted. Use `--max-chars` (default 48,000; minimum 4,000) to bound the result.

+# F05 project capsule episode
+
+Date: 2026-09-11
+
+## Bounded capability
+
+Added `python -m proteus capsule --project <existing-project> --out <capsule.md>`.
+It creates one bounded Markdown handoff artifact without writing to the selected
+project. The artifact records observable Git state, runtime manifests, required
+instructions, and prioritized excerpts of standard current-state and roadmap
+carriers. It is intentionally not an archive or transcript.
+
+## Exercise target
+
+Selected `C:\projects\multiagent-moderation-env` (selection rationale:
+`F05_PROJECT_SELECTION.md`). The generated artifact is
+`F05_MULTIAGENT_MODERATION_PROJECT_CAPSULE.md`.
+
+Final generation observation:
+
+- 38,527 Unicode characters (45,949 UTF-8 bytes), substantially smaller than
+  the target tree.
+- The target had 4,207 non-dependency/cache files by the capsule's inventory.
+- Git observation: `main` at `e52389098cb21d9d65a6998702253a7535e25872`,
+  dirty with 3,075 paths.
+- The capsule separated 324 non-cache-shaped paths from 2,751
+  browser/cache-shaped paths; it did not classify either group as safe to
+  delete.
+
+## Independent receiver
+
+An independent `codex exec --ephemeral` receiver was launched with no resume,
+fork, or prior transcript. Its only supplied project-context artifact was the
+first generated capsule; it was explicitly asked not to inspect source or
+modify the target. Input: `F05_CAPSULE_RECEIVER_PROMPT.txt`; raw preserved
+answer: `F05_CAPSULE_RECEIVER_RAW_RESULT.txt`; launch receipt:
+`F05_CAPSULE_RECEIVER_META.json`.
+
+The receiver correctly understood the product as a FastAPI/SQLAlchemy/Postgres
++ Next.js moderator workspace and identified dirty work, stale/conflicting
+roadmap signals, production LLM gating, and the need to inspect governing docs,
+diffs, owning modules, smoke scripts, and the master recovery plan before a
+change.
+
+## Causal repair after receiver review
+
+The first capsule's first-80 Git-path listing was dominated by browser profile
+and cache artifacts, obscuring the actionable dirty set. Also, the roadmap
+named `RECOVERY_REENTRY_MASTER_PLAN_v2_3_to_v3_4.md` as its source of truth but
+the capsule did not include it. The implementation now:
+
+1. summarizes browser/cache-shaped dirty paths separately while keeping an
+   explicit warning that they are not presumed disposable;
+2. prioritizes mandatory AGENTS/operations/product-law documents; and
+3. includes the named master recovery plan before the roadmap excerpt.
+
+The repaired final capsule was regenerated at the path above. The raw receiver
+answer remains untouched as the first-pass evaluation record.
+
+## Validation
+
+- `python tests/test_capsule.py` — PASS
+- `python -m pytest -q` — 50 passed
+- `python -m proteus capsule --help` — PASS
+- `git diff --check` — PASS (only line-ending warnings)
+
+## Known boundaries
+
+- Standard document names are intentionally prioritized; unusual project-local
+  state carriers require adding a candidate or inspecting source directly.
+- Redaction is heuristic and must never be treated as a permission to include
+  secrets in a capsule.
+- The capsule exposes Git paths and document excerpts, not diff bodies or
+  untracked-file contents. A receiver still must inspect relevant source before
+  editing.
+- The target's current-state documents contain historical and conflicting
+  sequencing. The capsule identifies this instead of selecting a false single
+  truth.

+"""Create a compact, source-grounded handoff capsule for a coding project.
+
+The capsule deliberately collects only durable entry material and observable git
+state.  It is not an archive and it does not attempt to infer a task from an
+agent transcript.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import re
+import subprocess
+from dataclasses import dataclass
+from datetime import datetime, timezone
+from pathlib import Path
+
+
+DEFAULT_MAX_CHARS = 48_000
+IGNORED_DIRS = {".git", ".next", "node_modules", ".venv", "__pycache__"}
+
+# Ordered from mandatory project instructions through likely current-work
+# carriers.  A missing file is normal; projects vary widely.
+DOCUMENT_CANDIDATES = (
+    ("Project instructions", "AGENTS.md", 3_000),
+    ("Project instructions", "CLAUDE.md", 3_000),
+    ("Operations constraints", "docs/ops/PRODUCTION_TOPOLOGY_AND_GATES.md", 4_500),
+    ("Product law", "docs/product/MODERATOR_COCKPIT_INTERFACE_LAW_v0_1.md", 5_000),
+    ("Project overview", "README.md", 3_000),
+    ("Current-state carrier", "CURRENT.md", 5_000),
+    ("Current-state carrier", "FRONTIER.md", 5_000),
+    ("Current-state carrier", "PROJECT_INTENT_AND_CURRENT_STATE.md", 7_000),
+    ("Current plan referenced by roadmap", "RECOVERY_REENTRY_MASTER_PLAN_v2_3_to_v3_4.md", 6_000),
+    ("Roadmap / next work", "ROADMAP_NEXT_STEPS.md", 5_000),
+    ("Handoff", "HANDOFF.md", 5_000),
+    ("Contribution guide", "CONTRIBUTING.md", 4_000),
+)
+MANIFEST_CANDIDATES = (
+    "pyproject.toml",
+    "package.json",
+    "requirements.txt",
+    "backend/requirements.txt",
+    "frontend/package.json",
+    "docker-compose.yml",
+    "docker-compose.yaml",
+    "Makefile",
+)
+
+
+def _run(project: Path, *args: str) -> tuple[int, str]:
+    try:
+        completed = subprocess.run(
+            args,
+            cwd=project,
+            capture_output=True,
+            text=True,
+            encoding="utf-8",
+            errors="replace",
+            timeout=20,
+        )
+    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as exc:
+        return 1, str(exc)
+    return completed.returncode, completed.stdout.strip()
+
+
+def _redact(text: str) -> str:
+    """Remove common credential forms before a capsule is written."""
+    patterns = (
+        r"(?i)(api[_-]?key|token|password|secret)\s*([=:])\s*[^\s'\"`]+",
+        r"\bsk-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{12,}\b",
+        r"\beyJ[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}\b",
+    )
+    for pattern in patterns:
+        text = re.sub(pattern, "<REDACTED>", text)
+    return text
+
+
+def _excerpt(path: Path, limit: int) -> str:
+    try:
+        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
+    except OSError as exc:
+        return f"[Could not read: {exc}]"
+    text = _redact(text).strip()
+    if len(text) <= limit:
+        return text
+    # Keep the beginning, which normally contains scope and instructions, and
+    # label the loss so a receiver does not mistake it for a complete document.
+    return text[:limit].rstrip() + f"\n\n[Excerpt truncated at {limit:,} characters; inspect {path.name} before relying on omitted detail.]"
+
+
+def _count_files(project: Path) -> int:
+    count = 0
+    for path in project.rglob("*"):
+        if any(part in IGNORED_DIRS for part in path.parts):
+            continue
+        if path.is_file():
+            count += 1
+    return count
+
+
+def _git_state(project: Path) -> tuple[list[str], str, str]:
+    code, root = _run(project, "git", "rev-parse", "--show-toplevel")
+    if code:
+        return [], "Not a Git repository (or git unavailable).", ""
+    _, branch = _run(project, "git", "branch", "--show-current")
+    _, head = _run(project, "git", "log", "-1", "--format=%H")
+    _, log = _run(project, "git", "log", "-8", "--date=short", "--format=%h %ad %s")
+    _, porcelain = _run(project, "git", "status", "--porcelain=v1", "-uall")
+    changed = porcelain.splitlines() if porcelain else []
+
+    def cache_shaped(entry: str) -> bool:
+        candidate = entry[3:].replace("\\", "/").lower()
+        return any(
+            marker in candidate
+            for marker in (
+                "/.edge-render-",
+                "/crashpad/",
+                "/cache/",
+                "/code cache/",
+                "/gpucache/",
+                "/dawngraphitecache/",
+                "/dawnwebgpucache/",
+            )
+        )
+
+    cache_paths = [entry for entry in changed if cache_shaped(entry)]
+    actionable = [entry for entry in changed if entry not in cache_paths]
+    status = [
+        f"Git root: `{root}`",
+        f"Branch: `{branch or '(detached HEAD)'}`",
+        f"HEAD: `{head or '(unavailable)'}`",
+        f"Working tree: {'clean' if not changed else f'dirty ({len(changed)} paths)'}",
+    ]
+    if changed:
+        status.append(
+            f"Changed-path triage: {len(actionable)} non-cache-shaped paths; "
+            f"{len(cache_paths)} browser/cache-shaped paths (not assumed disposable)."
+        )
+        shown = actionable[:80]
+        if shown:
+            status.append("Non-cache-shaped changed paths (porcelain; inspect before editing):\n```text\n" + "\n".join(shown) + "\n```")
+        if len(actionable) > len(shown):
+            status.append(f"[Only first {len(shown)} of {len(actionable)} non-cache-shaped paths shown.]")
+        if cache_paths:
+            status.append(
+                "[Browser/cache-shaped paths are summarized to keep the handoff legible; "
+                "they remain part of the dirty tree and must not be deleted blindly.]"
+            )
+    return status, log or "(No commits found.)", branch
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class CapsuleResult:
+    project: Path
+    output: Path
+    chars: int
+    included_documents: tuple[str, ...]
+
+
+def build_capsule(project: Path, output: Path, *, max_chars: int = DEFAULT_MAX_CHARS) -> CapsuleResult:
+    """Write one Markdown capsule.  Does not mutate *project*."""
+    project = project.expanduser().resolve()
+    output = output.expanduser().resolve()
+    if not project.is_dir():
+        raise ValueError(f"project is not a directory: {project}")
+    if max_chars < 4_000:
+        raise ValueError("max_chars must be at least 4000")
+
+    git_state, recent_log, branch = _git_state(project)
+    top_level = sorted(p.name + ("/" if p.is_dir() else "") for p in project.iterdir() if p.name not in IGNORED_DIRS)
+    lines = [
+        "# Project Capsule",
+        "",
+        "> Generated context, not a source of truth. Verify cited files and current Git state before changing the project.",
+        "",
+        "## Receiver protocol",
+        "",
+        "1. Treat the project instructions and constraints below as binding.",
+        "2. Preserve existing dirty work: inspect it before editing or cleaning anything.",
+        "3. Reconcile any conflicting roadmap/current-state material by checking Git history and the cited source files.",
+        "4. Before implementation, read the listed files most relevant to the chosen change and run the narrowest documented check.",
+        "",
+        "## Observed repository",
+        "",
+        f"- Project: `{project}`",
+        f"- Generated (UTC): `{datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()}`",
+        f"- Approximate tracked/working source files (excluding common dependency/cache directories): {_count_files(project):,}",
+        f"- Top level: {', '.join(f'`{name}`' for name in top_level[:60])}" + (" …" if len(top_level) > 60 else ""),
+        "",
+        "## Git working situation",
+        "",
+        *git_state,
+        "",
+        "### Recent commits",
+        "",
+        "```text",
+        recent_log,
+        "```",
+        "",
+        "## Runtime / build manifests",
+        "",
+    ]
+    for rel in MANIFEST_CANDIDATES:
+        path = project / rel
+        if path.is_file():
+            lines.extend((f"### `{rel}`", "", "```text", _excerpt(path, 3_000), "```", ""))
+
+    included: list[str] = []
+    # Reserve room for headings and the source map. Individual excerpts are
+    # additionally bounded; the global check below never silently overflows.
+    for category, rel, limit in DOCUMENT_CANDIDATES:
+        path = project / rel
+        if not path.is_file():
+            continue
+        remaining = max_chars - len("\n".join(lines)) - 1_600
+        if remaining < 500:
+            break
+        excerpt = _excerpt(path, min(limit, max(500, remaining)))
+        lines.extend((f"## {category}: `{rel}`", "", excerpt, ""))
+        included.append(rel)
+
+    lines.extend((
+        "## What to inspect before changing code",
+        "",
+        "- The mandatory instruction files above, plus any documents named by them for the surface you will change.",
+        "- The current diff for every dirty path that overlaps your task; do not assume it is disposable.",
+        "- The owning backend/frontend module and its nearest existing smoke or test script.",
+        "- Deployment/production gates before touching deploy, Caddy, provider configuration, or a manual-test gate.",
+        "",
+        "## Capsule boundaries",
+        "",
+        "- This is a compact index and excerpts, not an archive, a transcript, or a substitute for source inspection.",
+        "- It intentionally omits untracked file contents, Git diff bodies, binaries, dependency trees, and environment files.",
+        "- Common credential-shaped values are redacted heuristically; never treat a capsule as a safe place for secrets.",
+        "- `current` and `next` claims remain attributed to the included documents and Git observation; documents can be stale or conflict.",
+        "",
+        "## Included source map",
+        "",
+        *[f"- `{item}`" for item in included],
+    ))
+    text = "\n".join(lines).rstrip() + "\n"
+    if len(text) > max_chars:
+        # This should be rare because documents are budgeted. Preserve a valid,
+        # explicitly incomplete result instead of failing after expensive scan.
+        text = text[: max_chars - 120].rstrip() + "\n\n[Capsule reached global size limit; inspect project files directly.]\n"
+    output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    output.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
+    return CapsuleResult(project=project, output=output, chars=len(text), included_documents=tuple(included))

+                "chars": result.chars,
+                "included_documents": list(result.included_documents),
+            },
+            indent=2,
+        )
+    )
+

+"""Tests for compact, source-grounded project handoff capsules."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import os
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "src"))
+
+from proteus.capsule import build_capsule  # noqa: E402
+
+
+def run(project: Path, *args: str) -> None:
+    subprocess.run(args, cwd=project, check=True, capture_output=True, text=True)
+
+
+def test_capsule_contains_handoff_essentials_and_redacts() -> None:
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+        root = Path(td)
+        project = root / "project"
+        project.mkdir()
+        (project / "AGENTS.md").write_text("# Rules\nRead docs/law.md first.\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        (project / "README.md").write_text("# Example\nA practical application.\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        (project / "PROJECT_INTENT_AND_CURRENT_STATE.md").write_text(
+            "# Current\nThe active focus is migration safety.\nAPI_KEY=should_not_escape\n",
+            encoding="utf-8",
+        )
+        (project / "ROADMAP_NEXT_STEPS.md").write_text(
+            "# Next\nRun the targeted migration smoke before a broad suite.\n",
+            encoding="utf-8",
+        )
+        (project / "package.json").write_text('{"name":"example"}\n', encoding="utf-8")
+        (project / "changed.py").write_text("before\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        run(project, "git", "init")
+        run(project, "git", "config", "user.email", "test@example.invalid")
+        run(project, "git", "config", "user.name", "Capsule Test")
+        run(project, "git", "add", ".")
+        run(project, "git", "commit", "-m", "initial")
+        (project / "changed.py").write_text("after\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        (project / "untracked.txt").write_text("local work\n", encoding="utf-8")
+
+        out = root / "capsule.md"
+        result = build_capsule(project, out, max_chars=12_000)
+        text = out.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+        assert result.output == out.resolve()
+        assert result.chars == len(text)
+        assert "Read docs/law.md first" in text
+        assert "active focus is migration safety" in text
+        assert "targeted migration smoke" in text
+        assert "Working tree: dirty (2 paths)" in text
+        assert "Changed-path triage: 2 non-cache-shaped paths" in text
+        assert "changed.py" in text and "untracked.txt" in text
+        assert "should_not_escape" not in text
+        assert "<REDACTED>" in text
+        assert "What to inspect before changing code" in text
+
+
+def test_capsule_respects_global_bound_and_does_not_mutate_project() -> None:
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+        root = Path(td)
+        project = root / "project"
+        project.mkdir()
+        original = "# Readme\n" + ("useful context\n" * 2_000)
+        (project / "README.md").write_text(original, encoding="utf-8")
+        out = root / "capsule.md"
+        result = build_capsule(project, out, max_chars=4_500)
+        assert result.chars <= 4_500
+        assert (project / "README.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8") == original
+        assert "Generated context, not a source of truth" in out.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+
+
+def test_capsule_summarizes_cache_shaped_dirty_paths() -> None:
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+        root = Path(td)
+        project = root / "project"
+        cache = project / "SAS" / ".edge-render-x" / "Default" / "Cache"
+        cache.mkdir(parents=True)
+        (project / "README.md").write_text("# Example\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        (project / "useful.txt").write_text("keep me\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        cache_file = cache / "blob"
+        cache_file.write_text("generated\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        run(project, "git", "init")
+        run(project, "git", "config", "user.email", "test@example.invalid")
+        run(project, "git", "config", "user.name", "Capsule Test")
+        run(project, "git", "add", ".")
+        run(project, "git", "commit", "-m", "initial")
+        (project / "useful.txt").write_text("changed\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        cache_file.write_text("changed cache\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        out = root / "capsule.md"
+        build_capsule(project, out)
+        text = out.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+        assert "1 non-cache-shaped paths; 1 browser/cache-shaped paths" in text
+        assert "useful.txt" in text
+        assert "Browser/cache-shaped paths are summarized" in text
+
+
+def main() -> None:
+    test_capsule_contains_handoff_essentials_and_redacts()
+    test_capsule_respects_global_bound_and_does_not_mutate_project()
+    test_capsule_summarizes_cache_shaped_dirty_paths()
+    print("test_capsule: PASS")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()

# F05 project capsule episode

Date: 2026-09-11

## Bounded capability

Added `python -m proteus capsule --project <existing-project> --out <capsule.md>`.
It creates one bounded Markdown handoff artifact without writing to the selected
project. The artifact records observable Git state, runtime manifests, required
instructions, and prioritized excerpts of standard current-state and roadmap
carriers. It is intentionally not an archive or transcript.

## Exercise target

Selected `C:\projects\multiagent-moderation-env` (selection rationale:
`F05_PROJECT_SELECTION.md`). The generated artifact is
`F05_MULTIAGENT_MODERATION_PROJECT_CAPSULE.md`.

Final generation observation:

- 38,527 Unicode characters (45,949 UTF-8 bytes), substantially smaller than
  the target tree.
- The target had 4,207 non-dependency/cache files by the capsule's inventory.
- Git observation: `main` at `e52389098cb21d9d65a6998702253a7535e25872`,
  dirty with 3,075 paths.
- The capsule separated 324 non-cache-shaped paths from 2,751
  browser/cache-shaped paths; it did not classify either group as safe to
  delete.

## Independent receiver

An independent `codex exec --ephemeral` receiver was launched with no resume,
fork, or prior transcript. Its only supplied project-context artifact was the
first generated capsule; it was explicitly asked not to inspect source or
modify the target. Input: `F05_CAPSULE_RECEIVER_PROMPT.txt`; raw preserved
answer: `F05_CAPSULE_RECEIVER_RAW_RESULT.txt`; launch receipt:
`F05_CAPSULE_RECEIVER_META.json`.

The receiver correctly understood the product as a FastAPI/SQLAlchemy/Postgres
+ Next.js moderator workspace and identified dirty work, stale/conflicting
roadmap signals, production LLM gating, and the need to inspect governing docs,
diffs, owning modules, smoke scripts, and the master recovery plan before a
change.

## Causal repair after receiver review

The first capsule's first-80 Git-path listing was dominated by browser profile
and cache artifacts, obscuring the actionable dirty set. Also, the roadmap
named `RECOVERY_REENTRY_MASTER_PLAN_v2_3_to_v3_4.md` as its source of truth but
the capsule did not include it. The implementation now:

1. summarizes browser/cache-shaped dirty paths separately while keeping an
   explicit warning that they are not presumed disposable;
2. prioritizes mandatory AGENTS/operations/product-law documents; and
3. includes the named master recovery plan before the roadmap excerpt.

The repaired final capsule was regenerated at the path above. The raw receiver
answer remains untouched as the first-pass evaluation record.

## Validation

- `python tests/test_capsule.py` вЂ” PASS
- `python -m pytest -q` вЂ” 50 passed
- `python -m proteus capsule --help` вЂ” PASS
- `git diff --check` вЂ” PASS (only line-ending warnings)

## Known boundaries

- Standard document names are intentionally prioritized; unusual project-local
  state carriers require adding a candidate or inspecting source directly.
- Redaction is heuristic and must never be treated as a permission to include
  secrets in a capsule.
- The capsule exposes Git paths and document excerpts, not diff bodies or
  untracked-file contents. A receiver still must inspect relevant source before
  editing.
- The target's current-state documents contain historical and conflicting
  sequencing. The capsule identifies this instead of selecting a false single
  truth.

 (`PYTHONPATH=src` if needed.) Writes Proteus-owned surfaces and `.proteus/ENTRY.md`; does not overwrite an existing `AGENTS.md` or non-`proteus-*` Cursor rules. Not Gate 1.
+
+Project capsule (compact, practical context for a fresh coding agent):
+
+```
+python -m proteus capsule --project C:\path\to\project --out C:\handoff\PROJECT_CAPSULE.md
+```
+
+The command is read-only with respect to the selected project. It records observed Git state, manifests, mandatory instructions, and bounded excerpts from standard overview/current-state/roadmap documents. Give the Markdown file to the fresh agent as project context, then have it inspect the cited sources before editing. It deliberately omits diff bodies, untracked file contents, binaries, dependency trees, environment files, and agent transcripts; credential-shaped strings in included excerpts are heuristically redacted. Use `--max-chars` (default 48,000; minimum 4,000) to bound the result.
